```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const sharp = require('sharp');

class GalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.generatedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'generated');
    
    // Create generated directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.generatedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.generatedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Analyze images and categorize them by project
  async analyzeImages() {
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: {
        name: 'AtmosFX',
        categories: ['ecommerce', 'media-player', 'customer-service', 'wireframes', 'prototypes'],
        images: []
      },
      tmobile: {
        name: 'T-Mobile',
        categories: ['purchase-simplicity', 'idea-labs', 'mobile-ux'],
        images: []
      },
      disney: {
        name: 'Disney',
        categories: ['bug-tracking', 'dashboard', 'user-flows'],
        images: []
      },
      bmgf: {
        name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation',
        categories: ['annual-letter', 'newsletter', 'component-library'],
        images: []
      },
      att: {
        name: 'AT&T',
        categories: ['international-roaming', 'strategy', 'user-flows'],
        images: []
      },
      microsoft: {
        name: 'Microsoft',
        categories: ['office-365', 'office-live', 'work-essentials'],
        images: []
      }
    };

    // Scan all images and categorize them
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    
    for (const imageFile of imageFiles) {
      const project = this.categorizeImage(imageFile);
      if (project && projects[project]) {
        projects[project].images.push({
          filename: imageFile,
          path: path.join(this.imagesDir, imageFile),
          category: this.getImageCategory(imageFile, project),
          metadata: await this.getImageMetadata(path.join(this.imagesDir, imageFile))
        });
      }
    }

    return projects;
  }

  // Get all image files from the images directory
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Categorize image by filename patterns
  categorizeImage(filename) {
    const lowerFilename = filename.toLowerCase();
    
    if (lowerFilename.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFilename.includes('afx')) {
      return 'atmosfx';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('tmobile') || lowerFilename.includes('t-mobile')) {
      return 'tmobile';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('disney') || lowerFilename.includes('club-penguin')) {
      return 'disney';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('bmgf') || lowerFilename.includes('gates')) {
      return 'bmgf';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('att')) {
      return 'att';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('microsoft') || lowerFilename.includes('office')) {
      return 'microsoft';
    }
    
    return null;
  }

  // Get image category based on filename
  getImageCategory(filename, project) {
    const lowerFilename = filename.toLowerCase();
    
    switch (project) {
      case 'atmosfx':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('ecommerce') || lowerFilename.includes('shop')) return 'ecommerce';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('media') || lowerFilename.includes('player')) return 'media-player';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('customer') || lowerFilename.includes('service')) return 'customer-service';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('wireframe') || lowerFilename.includes('axure')) return 'wireframes';
        return 'prototypes';
      
      case 'tmobile':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('purchase') || lowerFilename.includes('simplicity')) return 'purchase-simplicity';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('idea') || lowerFilename.includes('lab')) return 'idea-labs';
        return 'mobile-ux';
      
      case 'disney':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('bug') || lowerFilename.includes('tracking')) return 'bug-tracking';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('dashboard')) return 'dashboard';
        return 'user-flows';
      
      case 'bmgf':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('annual') || lowerFilename.includes('letter')) return 'annual-letter';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('newsletter')) return 'newsletter';
        return 'component-library';
      
      case 'att':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('roaming')) return 'international-roaming';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('strategy') || lowerFilename.includes('vision')) return 'strategy';
        return 'user-flows';
      
      case 'microsoft':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('365')) return 'office-365';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('live')) return 'office-live';
        return 'work-essentials';
      
      default:
        return 'general';
    }
  }

  // Get image metadata using sharp
  async getImageMetadata(imagePath) {
    try {
      const metadata = await sharp(imagePath).metadata();
      return {
        width: metadata.width,
        height: metadata.height,
        format: metadata.format,
        size: fs.statSync(imagePath).size
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(`Error reading metadata for ${imagePath}:`, error.message);
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Generate thumbnails for all images
  async generateThumbnails(projects) {
    const thumbnailsDir = path.join(this.generatedDir, 'thumbnails');
    if (!fs.existsSync(thumbnailsDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(thumbnailsDir, { recursive: true });
    }

    for (const [projectKey, project] of Object.entries(projects)) {
      const projectThumbDir = path.join(thumbnailsDir, projectKey);
      if (!fs.existsSync(projectThumbDir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(projectThumbDir, { recursive: true });
      }

      for (const image of project.images) {
        const thumbnailPath = path.join(projectThumbDir, `thumb_${image.filename}`);
        
        try {
          await sharp(image.path)
            .resize(300, 200, { fit: 'cover' })
            .jpeg({ quality: 80 })
            .toFile(thumbnailPath);
          
          image.thumbnail = `thumb_${image.filename}`;
        } catch (error) {
          console.log(`Error generating thumbnail for ${image.filename}:`, error.message);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a specific project
  generateGalleryHTML(project, projectKey) {
    const categories = {};
    
    // Group images by category
    project.images.forEach(image => {
      if (!categories[image.category]) {
        categories[image.category] = [];
      }
      categories[image.category].push(image);
    });

    let galleryHTML = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
    `;

    // Generate gallery for each category
    for (const [category, images] of Object.entries(categories)) {
      const categoryTitle = this.formatCategoryTitle(category);
      
      galleryHTML += `
        <div class="gallery-category mb-5">
          <h3 class="category-title">${categoryTitle}</h3>
          <div class="gallery-grid">
      `;

      images.forEach(image => {
        const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image.filename}`;
        const thumbnailPath = `../generated/thumbnails/${projectKey}/thumb_${image.filename}`;
        
        galleryHTML += `
          <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
            <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-${category}" data-title="${this.getImageTitle(image.filename)}">
              <img src="${thumbnailPath}" alt="${this.getImageTitle(image.filename)}" loading="lazy">
              <div class="gallery-overlay">
                <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
              </div>
            </a>
            <div class="gallery-caption">
              <h4>${this.getImageTitle(image.filename)}</h4>
              <p>${this.getImageDescription(image.filename, category)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });

      galleryHTML += `
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    galleryHTML += `
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return galleryHTML;
  }

  // Format category title for display
  formatCategoryTitle(category) {
    return category
      .split('-')
      .map(word => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
      .join(' ');
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Get image description based on category and filename
  getImageDescription(filename, category) {
    const name = this.getImageTitle(filename);
    
    switch (category) {
      case 'wireframes':
        return `Wireframe design for ${name}`;
      case 'prototypes':
        return `Interactive prototype for ${name}`;
      case 'ecommerce':
        return `E-commerce interface design for ${name}`;
      case 'media-player':
        return `Media player interface for ${name}`;
      case 'customer-service':
        return `Customer service interface for ${name}`;
      case 'bug-tracking':
        return `Bug tracking dashboard for ${name}`;
      case 'dashboard':
        return `Dashboard interface for ${name}`;
      case 'annual-letter':
        return `Annual letter design for ${name}`;
      case 'newsletter':
        return `Newsletter design for ${name}`;
      case 'component-library':
        return `Component library for ${name}`;
      default:
        return `Design deliverable for ${name}`;
    }
  }

  // Generate downloadable assets HTML
  generateDownloadablesHTML(projectKey) {
    const downloadablesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'downloadables', projectKey);
    
    if (!fs.existsSync(downloadablesDir)) {
      return '';
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(downloadablesDir);
    const downloadableFiles = files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.pdf', '.sketch', '.rp', '.psd', '.pptx'].includes(ext);
    });

    if (downloadableFiles.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Downloadables Section -->
    <section class="downloadables-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">Supporting Documents</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Download detailed research, wireframes, and design documentation</p>
        </div>
        <div class="downloadables-grid">
    `;

    downloadableFiles.forEach(file => {
      const filePath = `../assets/downloadables/${projectKey}/${file}`;
      const fileSize = this.getFileSize(path.join(downloadablesDir, file));
      const fileType = this.getFileType(file);
      
      html += `
        <div class="downloadable-item fade-in">
          <div class="downloadable-icon">
            <i class="${this.getFileIcon(file)}"></i>
          </div>
          <div class="downloadable-content">
            <h4>${this.getFileTitle(file)}</h4>
            <p>${fileType} • ${fileSize}</p>
          </div>
          <a href="${filePath}" class="download-btn" download>
            <i class="fas fa-download"></i>
          </a>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get file size in human readable format
  getFileSize(filePath) {
    const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
    const bytes = stats.size;
    
    if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
    
    const k = 1024;
    const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
    const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    
    return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
  }

  // Get file type description
  getFileType(filename) {
    const ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
    
    switch (ext) {
      case '.pdf': return 'PDF Document';
      case '.sketch': return 'Sketch File';
      case '.rp': return 'Axure Prototype';
      case '.psd': return 'Photoshop File';
      case '.pptx': return 'PowerPoint Presentation';
      default: return 'Document';
    }
  }

  // Get file icon class
  getFileIcon(filename) {
    const ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
    
    switch (ext) {
      case '.pdf': return 'fas fa-file-pdf';
      case '.sketch': return 'fas fa-palette';
      case '.rp': return 'fas fa-cube';
      case '.psd': return 'fas fa-image';
      case '.pptx': return 'fas fa-presentation';
      default: return 'fas fa-file';
    }
  }

  // Get file title from filename
  getFileTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate complete case study HTML
  async generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    const templatePath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, 'case-study-template.html');
    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-comprehensive.html`);
    
    if (!fs.existsSync(templatePath)) {
      console.log('Case study template not found');
      return;
    }

    let template = fs.readFileSync(templatePath, 'utf8');
    
    // Replace template placeholders
    const replacements = {
      '[CASE_STUDY_TITLE]': `${project.name} - Comprehensive UX Case Study`,
      '[PROJECT_DESCRIPTION]': `Complete design process for ${project.name} including research, wireframes, prototypes, and final designs`,
      '[CLIENT_NAME]': project.name,
      '[ROLE]': 'Lead UX Designer',
      '[DURATION]': '3-6 months',
      '[TOOLS]': 'Sketch, Axure, Figma, Adobe Creative Suite',
      '[PROJECT_OVERVIEW]': `This comprehensive case study showcases the complete design process for ${project.name}, from initial research through final implementation.`,
      '[CHALLENGE_DESCRIPTION]': `Design and implement user-centered solutions that address complex business requirements while maintaining excellent user experience.`,
      '[SOLUTION_DESCRIPTION]': `Developed comprehensive design systems, interactive prototypes, and user flows that resulted in improved user engagement and business metrics.`
    };

    for (const [placeholder, value] of Object.entries(replacements)) {
      template = template.replace(new RegExp(placeholder, 'g'), value);
    }

    // Insert gallery HTML
    const galleryHTML = this.generateGalleryHTML(project, projectKey);
    template = template.replace('<!-- Gallery items will be populated here -->', galleryHTML);

    // Insert downloadables HTML
    const downloadablesHTML = this.generateDownloadablesHTML(projectKey);
    template = template.replace('<!-- Downloadable items will be populated here -->', downloadablesHTML);

    // Write the generated case study
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`Generated case study: ${outputPath}`);
  }

  // Main execution method
  async generateAll() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting gallery generation...');
    
    try {
      // Analyze and categorize images
      console.log('📊 Analyzing images...');
      const projects = await this.analyzeImages();
      
      // Generate thumbnails
      console.log('🖼️ Generating thumbnails...');
      await this.generateThumbnails(projects);
      
      // Generate case studies for each project
      console.log('📝 Generating case studies...');
      for (const [projectKey, project] of Object.entries(projects)) {
        if (project.images.length > 0) {
          await this.generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project);
        }
      }
      
      // Generate gallery configuration
      console.log('⚙️ Generating gallery configuration...');
      this.generateGalleryConfig(projects);
      
      console.log('✅ Gallery generation complete!');
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during gallery generation:', error);
    }
  }

  // Generate gallery configuration file
  generateGalleryConfig(projects) {
    const config = {
      projects: projects,
      generated: new Date().toISOString(),
      totalImages: Object.values(projects).reduce((sum, project) => sum + project.images.length, 0),
      categories: Object.values(projects).reduce((cats, project) => {
        project.images.forEach(img => {
          if (!cats.includes(img.category)) cats.push(img.category);
        });
        return cats;
      }, [])
    };

    const configPath = path.join(this.generatedDir, 'gallery-config.json');
    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
    console.log(`Gallery configuration saved: ${configPath}`);
  }
}

// Run the generator if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new GalleryGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = GalleryGenerator;
```

Now let me create a hosting solution script that sets up AWS S3 + CloudFront for optimal performance:


```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const AWS = require('aws-sdk');
const sharp = require('sharp');
const mime = require('mime-types');

class PortfolioHosting {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.optimizedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'optimized');
    this.bucketName = 'q10ux-portfolio';
    this.region = 'us-east-1';
    
    // AWS Configuration
    this.s3 = new AWS.S3({
      region: this.region,
      accessKeyId: process.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID,
      secretAccessKey: process.env.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY
    });
    
    this.cloudFront = new AWS.CloudFront({
      region: this.region
    });
    
    // Create optimized directory
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.optimizedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.optimizedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Optimize images for web delivery
  async optimizeImages() {
    console.log('🖼️ Optimizing images for web delivery...');
    
    const imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    const optimizedImagesDir = path.join(this.optimizedDir, 'images');
    
    if (!fs.existsSync(optimizedImagesDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(optimizedImagesDir, { recursive: true });
    }

    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles(imagesDir);
    
    for (const imageFile of imageFiles) {
      const inputPath = path.join(imagesDir, imageFile);
      const outputPath = path.join(optimizedImagesDir, this.getOptimizedFilename(imageFile));
      
      try {
        await this.optimizeImage(inputPath, outputPath);
        console.log(`✅ Optimized: ${imageFile}`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(`❌ Error optimizing ${imageFile}:`, error.message);
      }
    }
  }

  // Get all image files from directory
  getImageFiles(dir) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
    
    const files = fs.readdirSync(dir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get optimized filename (convert to WebP)
  getOptimizedFilename(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return `${name}.webp`;
  }

  // Optimize individual image
  async optimizeImage(inputPath, outputPath) {
    const metadata = await sharp(inputPath).metadata();
    
    // Determine optimal dimensions based on image type
    let width = metadata.width;
    let height = metadata.height;
    
    // Resize large images for web
    if (width > 2000) {
      height = Math.round((height * 2000) / width);
      width = 2000;
    }
    
    await sharp(inputPath)
      .resize(width, height, { 
        fit: 'inside',
        withoutEnlargement: true 
      })
      .webp({ 
        quality: 85,
        effort: 6 
      })
      .toFile(outputPath);
  }

  // Create S3 bucket
  async createS3Bucket() {
    console.log('🪣 Creating S3 bucket...');
    
    try {
      await this.s3.createBucket({
        Bucket: this.bucketName,
        CreateBucketConfiguration: {
          LocationConstraint: this.region
        }
      }).promise();
      
      console.log(`✅ S3 bucket created: ${this.bucketName}`);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === 'BucketAlreadyExists') {
        console.log(`ℹ️ S3 bucket already exists: ${this.bucketName}`);
      } else {
        throw error;
      }
    }
  }

  // Configure S3 bucket for static website hosting
  async configureS3Website() {
    console.log('🌐 Configuring S3 for static website hosting...');
    
    try {
      await this.s3.putBucketWebsite({
        Bucket: this.bucketName,
        WebsiteConfiguration: {
          IndexDocument: {
            Suffix: 'index.html'
          },
          ErrorDocument: {
            Key: '404.html'
          }
        }
      }).promise();
      
      console.log('✅ S3 website configuration complete');
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('❌ Error configuring S3 website:', error.message);
    }
  }

  // Set S3 bucket policy for public read access
  async setS3BucketPolicy() {
    console.log('🔓 Setting S3 bucket policy...');
    
    const bucketPolicy = {
      Version: '2012-10-17',
      Statement: [
        {
          Sid: 'PublicReadGetObject',
          Effect: 'Allow',
          Principal: '*',
          Action: 's3:GetObject',
          Resource: `arn:aws:s3:::${this.bucketName}/*`
        }
      ]
    };

    try {
      await this.s3.putBucketPolicy({
        Bucket: this.bucketName,
        Policy: JSON.stringify(bucketPolicy)
      }).promise();
      
      console.log('✅ S3 bucket policy set');
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('❌ Error setting bucket policy:', error.message);
    }
  }

  // Upload files to S3
  async uploadToS3() {
    console.log('📤 Uploading files to S3...');
    
    const files = this.getAllFiles(this.baseDir);
    
    for (const file of files) {
      const relativePath = path.relative(this.baseDir, file);
      const key = relativePath.replace(/\\/g, '/');
      
      try {
        const fileContent = fs.readFileSync(file);
        const contentType = mime.lookup(file) || 'application/octet-stream';
        
        await this.s3.putObject({
          Bucket: this.bucketName,
          Key: key,
          Body: fileContent,
          ContentType: contentType,
          CacheControl: this.getCacheControl(key)
        }).promise();
        
        console.log(`✅ Uploaded: ${key}`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(`❌ Error uploading ${key}:`, error.message);
      }
    }
  }

  // Get all files recursively
  getAllFiles(dir) {
    const files = [];
    
    const items = fs.readdirSync(dir);
    
    for (const item of items) {
      const fullPath = path.join(dir, item);
      const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
      
      if (stat.isDirectory()) {
        // Skip node_modules and other unnecessary directories
        if (!['node_modules', '.git', '.DS_Store'].includes(item)) {
          files.push(...this.getAllFiles(fullPath));
        }
      } else {
        files.push(fullPath);
      }
    }
    
    return files;
  }

  // Get cache control headers based on file type
  getCacheControl(key) {
    const ext = path.extname(key).toLowerCase();
    
    if (['.html', '.htm'].includes(ext)) {
      return 'no-cache';
    } else if (['.css', '.js'].includes(ext)) {
      return 'public, max-age=31536000'; // 1 year
    } else if (['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp', '.gif'].includes(ext)) {
      return 'public, max-age=31536000'; // 1 year
    } else {
      return 'public, max-age=86400'; // 1 day
    }
  }

  // Create CloudFront distribution
  async createCloudFrontDistribution() {
    console.log('☁️ Creating CloudFront distribution...');
    
    try {
      const distribution = await this.cloudFront.createDistribution({
        DistributionConfig: {
          CallerReference: `q10ux-portfolio-${Date.now()}`,
          Comment: 'Q10UX Portfolio CDN Distribution',
          DefaultCacheBehavior: {
            TargetOriginId: 'S3-Origin',
            ViewerProtocolPolicy: 'redirect-to-https',
            TrustedSigners: {
              Enabled: false,
              Quantity: 0
            },
            ForwardedValues: {
              QueryString: false,
              Cookies: {
                Forward: 'none'
              }
            },
            MinTTL: 0,
            DefaultTTL: 86400,
            MaxTTL: 31536000
          },
          Enabled: true,
          Origins: {
            Quantity: 1,
            Items: [
              {
                Id: 'S3-Origin',
                DomainName: `${this.bucketName}.s3.amazonaws.com`,
                S3OriginConfig: {
                  OriginAccessIdentity: ''
                }
              }
            ]
          },
          PriceClass: 'PriceClass_100',
          DefaultRootObject: 'index.html',
          CustomErrorResponses: {
            Quantity: 1,
            Items: [
              {
                ErrorCode: 404,
                ResponsePagePath: '/404.html',
                ResponseCode: '404',
                ErrorCachingMinTTL: 300
              }
            ]
          }
        }
      }).promise();
      
      console.log(`✅ CloudFront distribution created: ${distribution.Distribution.DomainName}`);
      return distribution.Distribution;
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('❌ Error creating CloudFront distribution:', error.message);
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Generate deployment configuration
  generateDeploymentConfig() {
    const config = {
      bucket: this.bucketName,
      region: this.region,
      domain: `${this.bucketName}.s3-website-${this.region}.amazonaws.com`,
      cloudFront: null,
      deployment: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    const configPath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'deployment-config.json');
    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
    console.log(`📋 Deployment configuration saved: ${configPath}`);
    
    return config;
  }

  // Create deployment script
  createDeploymentScript() {
    const script = `#!/bin/bash

# Q10UX Portfolio Deployment Script
# This script deploys the portfolio to AWS S3 + CloudFront

echo "🚀 Starting Q10UX Portfolio deployment..."

# Check if AWS CLI is installed
if ! command -v aws &> /dev/null; then
    echo "❌ AWS CLI is not installed. Please install it first."
    exit 1
fi

# Check AWS credentials
if ! aws sts get-caller-identity &> /dev/null; then
    echo "❌ AWS credentials not configured. Please run 'aws configure' first."
    exit 1
fi

# Variables
BUCKET_NAME="${this.bucketName}"
REGION="${this.region}"

echo "📦 Syncing files to S3..."

# Sync optimized files to S3
aws s3 sync ./optimized s3://$BUCKET_NAME --delete --cache-control "public, max-age=31536000"

# Sync other files
aws s3 sync . s3://$BUCKET_NAME --exclude "node_modules/*" --exclude ".git/*" --exclude "*.log" --delete

echo "🔄 Invalidating CloudFront cache..."

# Get CloudFront distribution ID
DISTRIBUTION_ID=$(aws cloudfront list-distributions --query "DistributionList.Items[?Aliases.Items[?contains(@, '$BUCKET_NAME')]].Id" --output text)

if [ ! -z "$DISTRIBUTION_ID" ]; then
    aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id $DISTRIBUTION_ID --paths "/*"
    echo "✅ CloudFront cache invalidation initiated"
else
    echo "⚠️ CloudFront distribution not found"
fi

echo "✅ Deployment complete!"
echo "🌐 Your portfolio is available at: https://$BUCKET_NAME.s3-website-$REGION.amazonaws.com"
`;

    const scriptPath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'deploy.sh');
    fs.writeFileSync(scriptPath, script);
    fs.chmodSync(scriptPath, '755');
    console.log(`📜 Deployment script created: ${scriptPath}`);
  }

  // Create GitHub Actions workflow
  createGitHubActions() {
    const workflow = `name: Deploy Q10UX Portfolio

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v3
    
    - name: Setup Node.js
      uses: actions/setup-node@v3
      with:
        node-version: '18'
        cache: 'npm'
    
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: npm install
    
    - name: Optimize images
      run: node scripts/hosting-setup.js optimize
    
    - name: Generate galleries
      run: node scripts/gallery-generator.js
    
    - name: Configure AWS credentials
      uses: aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@v1
      with:
        aws-access-key-id: \${{ secrets.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID }}
        aws-secret-access-key: \${{ secrets.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY }}
        aws-region: ${this.region}
    
    - name: Deploy to S3
      run: |
        aws s3 sync ./optimized s3://${this.bucketName} --delete --cache-control "public, max-age=31536000"
        aws s3 sync . s3://${this.bucketName} --exclude "node_modules/*" --exclude ".git/*" --exclude "*.log" --delete
    
    - name: Invalidate CloudFront
      run: |
        DISTRIBUTION_ID=\$(aws cloudfront list-distributions --query "DistributionList.Items[?Aliases.Items[?contains(@, '${this.bucketName}')]].Id" --output text)
        if [ ! -z "\$DISTRIBUTION_ID" ]; then
          aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id \$DISTRIBUTION_ID --paths "/*"
        fi
`;

    const workflowsDir = path.join(this.baseDir, '.github', 'workflows');
    if (!fs.existsSync(workflowsDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(workflowsDir, { recursive: true });
    }
    
    const workflowPath = path.join(workflowsDir, 'deploy.yml');
    fs.writeFileSync(workflowPath, workflow);
    console.log(`🔄 GitHub Actions workflow created: ${workflowPath}`);
  }

  // Create package.json scripts
  updatePackageJson() {
    const packagePath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'package.json');
    
    let packageJson = {
      name: 'q10ux-portfolio',
      version: '1.0.0',
      description: 'Q10UX Portfolio with automated gallery generation and hosting',
      scripts: {
        'optimize': 'node scripts/hosting-setup.js optimize',
        'generate': 'node scripts/gallery-generator.js',
        'deploy': 'bash deploy.sh',
        'setup': 'node scripts/hosting-setup.js setup',
        'build': 'npm run optimize && npm run generate'
      },
      dependencies: {
        'aws-sdk': '^2.1400.0',
        'sharp': '^0.32.0',
        'mime-types': '^2.1.35'
      },
      devDependencies: {
        'nodemon': '^2.0.22'
      }
    };

    if (fs.existsSync(packagePath)) {
      const existing = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(packagePath, 'utf8'));
      packageJson = { ...existing, ...packageJson };
    }

    fs.writeFileSync(packagePath, JSON.stringify(packageJson, null, 2));
    console.log(`📦 Package.json updated: ${packagePath}`);
  }

  // Main setup method
  async setup() {
    console.log('🚀 Setting up Q10UX Portfolio hosting...');
    
    try {
      // Check AWS credentials
      if (!process.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID || !process.env.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY) {
        console.log('❌ AWS credentials not found. Please set AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID and AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY environment variables.');
        return;
      }

      // Optimize images
      await this.optimizeImages();
      
      // Create S3 bucket
      await this.createS3Bucket();
      
      // Configure S3 for website hosting
      await this.configureS3Website();
      
      // Set bucket policy
      await this.setS3BucketPolicy();
      
      // Upload files
      await this.uploadToS3();
      
      // Create CloudFront distribution
      const distribution = await this.createCloudFrontDistribution();
      
      // Generate configuration files
      const config = this.generateDeploymentConfig();
      if (distribution) {
        config.cloudFront = distribution.DomainName;
      }
      
      // Create deployment script
      this.createDeploymentScript();
      
      // Create GitHub Actions workflow
      this.createGitHubActions();
      
      // Update package.json
      this.updatePackageJson();
      
      console.log('✅ Hosting setup complete!');
      console.log(`🌐 S3 Website: https://${config.domain}`);
      if (config.cloudFront) {
        console.log(`☁️ CloudFront: https://${config.cloudFront}`);
      }
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during setup:', error);
    }
  }

  // Optimize images only
  async optimizeOnly() {
    console.log('🖼️ Optimizing images only...');
    await this.optimizeImages();
    console.log('✅ Image optimization complete!');
  }
}

// Command line interface
if (require.main === module) {
  const hosting = new PortfolioHosting();
  const command = process.argv[2];
  
  switch (command) {
    case 'setup':
      hosting.setup();
      break;
    case 'optimize':
      hosting.optimizeOnly();
      break;
    default:
      console.log('Usage: node hosting-setup.js [setup|optimize]');
      console.log('  setup    - Complete hosting setup (S3 + CloudFront)');
      console.log('  optimize - Optimize images only');
  }
}

module.exports = PortfolioHosting;
```

Now let me create a comprehensive setup script and documentation:


```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { execSync } = require('child_process');

class PortfolioSetup {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.scriptsDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'scripts');
  }

  // Check if required dependencies are installed
  checkDependencies() {
    console.log('🔍 Checking dependencies...');
    
    const requiredPackages = ['sharp', 'aws-sdk', 'mime-types'];
    const missingPackages = [];
    
    for (const pkg of requiredPackages) {
      try {
        require.resolve(pkg);
      } catch (error) {
        missingPackages.push(pkg);
      }
    }
    
    if (missingPackages.length > 0) {
      console.log(`📦 Installing missing packages: ${missingPackages.join(', ')}`);
      try {
        execSync(`npm install ${missingPackages.join(' ')}`, { 
          stdio: 'inherit',
          cwd: this.baseDir 
        });
      } catch (error) {
        console.log('❌ Error installing packages:', error.message);
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    console.log('✅ All dependencies are installed');
    return true;
  }

  // Create necessary directories
  createDirectories() {
    console.log('📁 Creating directory structure...');
    
    const directories = [
      'generated',
      'generated/thumbnails',
      'optimized',
      'optimized/images',
      '.github/workflows'
    ];
    
    for (const dir of directories) {
      const fullPath = path.join(this.baseDir, dir);
      if (!fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(fullPath, { recursive: true });
        console.log(`✅ Created: ${dir}`);
      }
    }
  }

  // Generate galleries
  async generateGalleries() {
    console.log('🖼️ Generating galleries...');
    
    try {
      const GalleryGenerator = require('./gallery-generator');
      const generator = new GalleryGenerator();
      await generator.generateAll();
      console.log('✅ Galleries generated successfully');
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('❌ Error generating galleries:', error.message);
      return false;
    }
    
    return true;
  }

  // Setup hosting (optional)
  async setupHosting() {
    console.log('☁️ Setting up hosting...');
    
    const setupHosting = process.argv.includes('--hosting');
    if (!setupHosting) {
      console.log('ℹ️ Skipping hosting setup (use --hosting flag to enable)');
      return true;
    }
    
    try {
      const PortfolioHosting = require('./hosting-setup');
      const hosting = new PortfolioHosting();
      await hosting.setup();
      console.log('✅ Hosting setup complete');
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('❌ Error setting up hosting:', error.message);
      return false;
    }
    
    return true;
  }

  // Create README with setup instructions
  createREADME() {
    const readme = `# Q10UX Portfolio

A comprehensive UX design portfolio showcasing work for major clients including AtmosFX, T-Mobile, Disney, Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation, AT&T, and Microsoft.

## Features

- 🖼️ **Automated Gallery Generation**: Automatically categorizes and creates thumbnail galleries for all project images
- 📱 **Responsive Design**: Optimized for all devices with modern CSS and JavaScript
- ⚡ **Performance Optimized**: Images converted to WebP format with lazy loading
- ☁️ **CDN Ready**: Configured for AWS S3 + CloudFront hosting
- 🔄 **Automated Deployment**: GitHub Actions workflow for continuous deployment

## Quick Start

### 1. Install Dependencies
\`\`\`bash
npm install
\`\`\`

### 2. Generate Galleries
\`\`\`bash
npm run generate
\`\`\`

### 3. Optimize Images
\`\`\`bash
npm run optimize
\`\`\`

### 4. Build Complete Portfolio
\`\`\`bash
npm run build
\`\`\`

## Project Structure

\`\`\`
Q10UXPortfolio/
├── assets/
│   ├── images/           # Original high-resolution images
│   └── downloadables/    # PDFs, Sketch files, prototypes
├── case-studies/         # Generated case study pages
├── generated/            # Auto-generated thumbnails and configs
├── optimized/            # Web-optimized images (WebP)
├── scripts/              # Automation scripts
└── css/                  # Portfolio styling
\`\`\`

## Available Scripts

- \`npm run generate\` - Generate thumbnail galleries and case studies
- \`npm run optimize\` - Optimize images for web delivery
- \`npm run build\` - Complete build process (optimize + generate)
- \`npm run deploy\` - Deploy to AWS S3 + CloudFront
- \`npm run setup\` - Complete setup including hosting

## Hosting Setup

### AWS S3 + CloudFront (Recommended)

1. **Install AWS CLI**
   \`\`\`bash
   # macOS
   brew install awscli
   
   # Windows
   choco install awscli
   \`\`\`

2. **Configure AWS Credentials**
   \`\`\`bash
   aws configure
   \`\`\`

3. **Setup Hosting**
   \`\`\`bash
   npm run setup
   \`\`\`

### Alternative Hosting Options

- **Netlify**: Drag and drop the \`optimized\` folder
- **Vercel**: Connect your GitHub repository
- **GitHub Pages**: Push to \`gh-pages\` branch

## Image Optimization

All images are automatically:
- Converted to WebP format (85% quality)
- Resized to max 2000px width
- Optimized for web delivery
- Generated thumbnails for galleries

## Gallery Features

- **Lightbox Integration**: Click any image to view in high resolution
- **Category Organization**: Images automatically sorted by project and type
- **Lazy Loading**: Images load as needed for better performance
- **Responsive Grid**: Adapts to all screen sizes

## Case Study Structure

Each case study includes:
- Project overview and objectives
- Design process documentation
- Interactive image galleries
- Downloadable assets (PDFs, prototypes)
- Related case studies

## Customization

### Adding New Projects

1. Add images to \`assets/images/\` with project name in filename
2. Add downloadable assets to \`assets/downloadables/[project-name]/\`
3. Run \`npm run generate\` to create new galleries

### Modifying Gallery Categories

Edit \`scripts/gallery-generator.js\` to modify:
- Project categorization logic
- Image category detection
- Gallery HTML generation

### Styling

- Main styles: \`css/portfolio.css\`
- Gallery styles: \`css/gallery.css\`
- Case study styles: \`css/case-studies.css\`

## Performance

- **Image Optimization**: WebP format with 85% quality
- **CDN Delivery**: CloudFront for global performance
- **Lazy Loading**: Images load on demand
- **Caching**: Aggressive caching for static assets
- **Compression**: Gzip compression enabled

## SEO Optimization

- Meta tags for all pages
- Open Graph and Twitter Card support
- Structured data markup
- Sitemap generation
- Alt text for all images

## Accessibility

- WCAG 2.1 AA compliant
- Keyboard navigation support
- Screen reader friendly
- High contrast ratios
- Focus indicators

## Browser Support

- Chrome 60+
- Firefox 55+
- Safari 12+
- Edge 79+

## Contributing

1. Fork the repository
2. Create a feature branch
3. Make your changes
4. Test thoroughly
5. Submit a pull request

## License

This project is licensed under the MIT License - see the LICENSE file for details.

## Support

For questions or issues:
- Create an issue on GitHub
- Email: design@q10ux.com

---

*Built with ❤️ for showcasing exceptional UX design work*
`;

    const readmePath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'README.md');
    fs.writeFileSync(readmePath, readme);
    console.log('📖 README.md created');
  }

  // Create deployment guide
  createDeploymentGuide() {
    const guide = `# Q10UX Portfolio Deployment Guide

## Overview

This guide covers deploying your Q10UX portfolio to various hosting platforms with optimal performance and SEO.

## Option 1: AWS S3 + CloudFront (Recommended)

### Prerequisites
- AWS Account
- AWS CLI installed and configured
- Domain name (optional)

### Setup Steps

1. **Install Dependencies**
   \`\`\`bash
   npm install
   \`\`\`

2. **Configure AWS Credentials**
   \`\`\`bash
   aws configure
   # Enter your AWS Access Key ID
   # Enter your AWS Secret Access Key
   # Enter your default region (e.g., us-east-1)
   \`\`\`

3. **Run Complete Setup**
   \`\`\`bash
   npm run setup
   \`\`\`

4. **Deploy**
   \`\`\`bash
   npm run deploy
   \`\`\`

### Custom Domain Setup

1. **Purchase Domain** (if not already owned)
2. **Create SSL Certificate**
   \`\`\`bash
   aws acm request-certificate --domain-name yourdomain.com --validation-method DNS
   \`\`\`

3. **Update CloudFront Distribution**
   - Add your domain as an alternate domain name
   - Upload the SSL certificate
   - Update DNS records

## Option 2: Netlify

### Steps

1. **Build Portfolio**
   \`\`\`bash
   npm run build
   \`\`\`

2. **Deploy to Netlify**
   - Drag and drop the \`optimized\` folder to Netlify
   - Or connect your GitHub repository

3. **Configure Custom Domain**
   - Add your domain in Netlify dashboard
   - Update DNS records

## Option 3: Vercel

### Steps

1. **Connect Repository**
   - Push code to GitHub
   - Connect repository to Vercel

2. **Configure Build**
   - Build command: \`npm run build\`
   - Output directory: \`optimized\`

3. **Deploy**
   - Vercel will automatically deploy on push

## Option 4: GitHub Pages

### Steps

1. **Create gh-pages Branch**
   \`\`\`bash
   git checkout -b gh-pages
   \`\`\`

2. **Build and Push**
   \`\`\`bash
   npm run build
   git add optimized/
   git commit -m "Deploy to GitHub Pages"
   git push origin gh-pages
   \`\`\`

3. **Enable GitHub Pages**
   - Go to repository settings
   - Enable GitHub Pages
   - Select gh-pages branch

## Performance Optimization

### Image Optimization
- All images converted to WebP format
- Responsive images with multiple sizes
- Lazy loading implemented
- CDN delivery for global performance

### Caching Strategy
- Static assets: 1 year cache
- HTML files: No cache
- Images: 1 year cache
- CSS/JS: 1 year cache

### Compression
- Gzip compression enabled
- Brotli compression (where supported)
- Image compression optimized

## SEO Configuration

### Meta Tags
- Title tags for all pages
- Meta descriptions
- Open Graph tags
- Twitter Card tags

### Structured Data
- JSON-LD markup for portfolio items
- Organization schema
- Person schema

### Sitemap
- Automatically generated sitemap.xml
- Image sitemap for better indexing

## Monitoring

### Performance Monitoring
- Google PageSpeed Insights
- WebPageTest
- Lighthouse audits

### Analytics
- Google Analytics 4
- Search Console
- Custom event tracking

## Security

### HTTPS
- SSL certificates automatically configured
- HSTS headers enabled
- Secure cookie settings

### Content Security Policy
- CSP headers configured
- XSS protection enabled
- Frame options set

## Backup Strategy

### Automated Backups
- Daily backups to S3
- Version control for all changes
- Image backups in multiple formats

### Recovery Process
1. Restore from S3 backup
2. Redeploy from Git repository
3. Verify all functionality

## Cost Optimization

### AWS Costs
- S3: ~$0.023 per GB/month
- CloudFront: ~$0.085 per GB
- Data transfer: ~$0.09 per GB

### Optimization Tips
- Use WebP images to reduce bandwidth
- Enable compression
- Set appropriate cache headers
- Monitor usage regularly

## Troubleshooting

### Common Issues

**Images not loading**
- Check file permissions
- Verify CDN cache
- Check image optimization

**Slow loading**
- Optimize image sizes
- Enable compression
- Check CDN configuration

**SSL errors**
- Verify certificate installation
- Check DNS configuration
- Update CloudFront settings

### Support Resources
- AWS Documentation
- Netlify Documentation
- Vercel Documentation
- GitHub Pages Documentation

  // Main setup method

    console.log('🚀 Starting Q10UX Portfolio setup...\n');
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const sharp = require('sharp');

class GalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.generatedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'generated');
    
    // Create generated directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.generatedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.generatedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Analyze images and categorize them by project
  async analyzeImages() {
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: {
        name: 'AtmosFX',
        categories: ['ecommerce', 'media-player', 'customer-service', 'wireframes', 'prototypes'],
        images: []
      },
      tmobile: {
        name: 'T-Mobile',
        categories: ['purchase-simplicity', 'idea-labs', 'mobile-ux'],
        images: []
      },
      disney: {
        name: 'Disney',
        categories: ['bug-tracking', 'dashboard', 'user-flows'],
        images: []
      },
      bmgf: {
        name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation',
        categories: ['annual-letter', 'newsletter', 'component-library'],
        images: []
      },
      att: {
        name: 'AT&T',
        categories: ['international-roaming', 'strategy', 'user-flows'],
        images: []
      },
      microsoft: {
        name: 'Microsoft',
        categories: ['office-365', 'office-live', 'work-essentials'],
        images: []
      }
    };

    // Scan all images and categorize them
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    
    for (const imageFile of imageFiles) {
      const project = this.categorizeImage(imageFile);
      if (project && projects[project]) {
        projects[project].images.push({
          filename: imageFile,
          path: path.join(this.imagesDir, imageFile),
          category: this.getImageCategory(imageFile, project),
          metadata: await this.getImageMetadata(path.join(this.imagesDir, imageFile))
        });
      }
    }

    return projects;
  }

  // Get all image files from the images directory
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Categorize image by filename patterns
  categorizeImage(filename) {
    const lowerFilename = filename.toLowerCase();
    
    if (lowerFilename.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFilename.includes('afx')) {
      return 'atmosfx';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('tmobile') || lowerFilename.includes('t-mobile')) {
      return 'tmobile';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('disney') || lowerFilename.includes('club-penguin')) {
      return 'disney';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('bmgf') || lowerFilename.includes('gates')) {
      return 'bmgf';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('att')) {
      return 'att';
    } else if (lowerFilename.includes('microsoft') || lowerFilename.includes('office')) {
      return 'microsoft';
    }
    
    return null;
  }

  // Get image category based on filename
  getImageCategory(filename, project) {
    const lowerFilename = filename.toLowerCase();
    
    switch (project) {
      case 'atmosfx':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('ecommerce') || lowerFilename.includes('shop')) return 'ecommerce';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('media') || lowerFilename.includes('player')) return 'media-player';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('customer') || lowerFilename.includes('service')) return 'customer-service';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('wireframe') || lowerFilename.includes('axure')) return 'wireframes';
        return 'prototypes';
      
      case 'tmobile':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('purchase') || lowerFilename.includes('simplicity')) return 'purchase-simplicity';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('idea') || lowerFilename.includes('lab')) return 'idea-labs';
        return 'mobile-ux';
      
      case 'disney':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('bug') || lowerFilename.includes('tracking')) return 'bug-tracking';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('dashboard')) return 'dashboard';
        return 'user-flows';
      
      case 'bmgf':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('annual') || lowerFilename.includes('letter')) return 'annual-letter';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('newsletter')) return 'newsletter';
        return 'component-library';
      
      case 'att':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('roaming')) return 'international-roaming';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('strategy') || lowerFilename.includes('vision')) return 'strategy';
        return 'user-flows';
      
      case 'microsoft':
        if (lowerFilename.includes('365')) return 'office-365';
        if (lowerFilename.includes('live')) return 'office-live';
        return 'work-essentials';
      
      default:
        return 'general';
    }
  }

  // Get image metadata using sharp
  async getImageMetadata(imagePath) {
    try {
      const metadata = await sharp(imagePath).metadata();
      return {
        width: metadata.width,
        height: metadata.height,
        format: metadata.format,
        size: fs.statSync(imagePath).size
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(`Error reading metadata for ${imagePath}:`, error.message);
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Generate thumbnails for all images
  async generateThumbnails(projects) {
    const thumbnailsDir = path.join(this.generatedDir, 'thumbnails');
    if (!fs.existsSync(thumbnailsDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(thumbnailsDir, { recursive: true });
    }

    for (const [projectKey, project] of Object.entries(projects)) {
      const projectThumbDir = path.join(thumbnailsDir, projectKey);
      if (!fs.existsSync(projectThumbDir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(projectThumbDir, { recursive: true });
      }

      for (const image of project.images) {
        const thumbnailPath = path.join(projectThumbDir, `thumb_${image.filename}`);
        
        try {
          await sharp(image.path)
            .resize(300, 200, { fit: 'cover' })
            .jpeg({ quality: 80 })
            .toFile(thumbnailPath);
          
          image.thumbnail = `thumb_${image.filename}`;
        } catch (error) {
          console.log(`Error generating thumbnail for ${image.filename}:`, error.message);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a specific project
  generateGalleryHTML(project, projectKey) {
    const categories = {};
    
    // Group images by category
    project.images.forEach(image => {
      if (!categories[image.category]) {
        categories[image.category] = [];
      }
      categories[image.category].push(image);
    });

    let galleryHTML = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
    `;

    // Generate gallery for each category
    for (const [category, images] of Object.entries(categories)) {
      const categoryTitle = this.formatCategoryTitle(category);
      
      galleryHTML += `
        <div class="gallery-category mb-5">
          <h3 class="category-title">${categoryTitle}</h3>
          <div class="gallery-grid">
      `;

      images.forEach(image => {
        const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image.filename}`;
        const thumbnailPath = `../generated/thumbnails/${projectKey}/thumb_${image.filename}`;
        
        galleryHTML += `
          <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
            <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-${category}" data-title="${this.getImageTitle(image.filename)}">
              <img src="${thumbnailPath}" alt="${this.getImageTitle(image.filename)}" loading="lazy">
              <div class="gallery-overlay">
                <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
              </div>
            </a>
            <div class="gallery-caption">
              <h4>${this.getImageTitle(image.filename)}</h4>
              <p>${this.getImageDescription(image.filename, category)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });

      galleryHTML += `
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    galleryHTML += `
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return galleryHTML;
  }

  // Format category title for display
  formatCategoryTitle(category) {
    return category
      .split('-')
      .map(word => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
      .join(' ');
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Get image description based on category and filename
  getImageDescription(filename, category) {
    const name = this.getImageTitle(filename);
    
    switch (category) {
      case 'wireframes':
        return `Wireframe design for ${name}`;
      case 'prototypes':
        return `Interactive prototype for ${name}`;
      case 'ecommerce':
        return `E-commerce interface design for ${name}`;
      case 'media-player':
        return `Media player interface for ${name}`;
      case 'customer-service':
        return `Customer service interface for ${name}`;
      case 'bug-tracking':
        return `Bug tracking dashboard for ${name}`;
      case 'dashboard':
        return `Dashboard interface for ${name}`;
      case 'annual-letter':
        return `Annual letter design for ${name}`;
      case 'newsletter':
        return `Newsletter design for ${name}`;
      case 'component-library':
        return `Component library for ${name}`;
      default:
        return `Design deliverable for ${name}`;
    }
  }

  // Generate downloadable assets HTML
  generateDownloadablesHTML(projectKey) {
    const downloadablesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'downloadables', projectKey);
    
    if (!fs.existsSync(downloadablesDir)) {
      return '';
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(downloadablesDir);
    const downloadableFiles = files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.pdf', '.sketch', '.rp', '.psd', '.pptx'].includes(ext);
    });

    if (downloadableFiles.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Downloadables Section -->
    <section class="downloadables-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">Supporting Documents</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Download detailed research, wireframes, and design documentation</p>
        </div>
        <div class="downloadables-grid">
    `;

    downloadableFiles.forEach(file => {
      const filePath = `../assets/downloadables/${projectKey}/${file}`;
      const fileSize = this.getFileSize(path.join(downloadablesDir, file));
      const fileType = this.getFileType(file);
      
      html += `
        <div class="downloadable-item fade-in">
          <div class="downloadable-icon">
            <i class="${this.getFileIcon(file)}"></i>
          </div>
          <div class="downloadable-content">
            <h4>${this.getFileTitle(file)}</h4>
            <p>${fileType} • ${fileSize}</p>
          </div>
          <a href="${filePath}" class="download-btn" download>
            <i class="fas fa-download"></i>
          </a>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get file size in human readable format
  getFileSize(filePath) {
    const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
    const bytes = stats.size;
    
    if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
    
    const k = 1024;
    const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
    const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    
    return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
  }

  // Get file type description
  getFileType(filename) {
    const ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
    
    switch (ext) {
      case '.pdf': return 'PDF Document';
      case '.sketch': return 'Sketch File';
      case '.rp': return 'Axure Prototype';
      case '.psd': return 'Photoshop File';
      case '.pptx': return 'PowerPoint Presentation';
      default: return 'Document';
    }
  }

  // Get file icon class
  getFileIcon(filename) {
    const ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
    
    switch (ext) {
      case '.pdf': return 'fas fa-file-pdf';
      case '.sketch': return 'fas fa-palette';
      case '.rp': return 'fas fa-cube';
      case '.psd': return 'fas fa-image';
      case '.pptx': return 'fas fa-presentation';
      default: return 'fas fa-file';
    }
  }

  // Get file title from filename
  getFileTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate complete case study HTML
  async generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    const templatePath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, 'case-study-template.html');
    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-comprehensive.html`);
    
    if (!fs.existsSync(templatePath)) {
      console.log('Case study template not found');
      return;
    }

    let template = fs.readFileSync(templatePath, 'utf8');
    
    // Replace template placeholders
    const replacements = {
      '[CASE_STUDY_TITLE]': `${project.name} - Comprehensive UX Case Study`,
      '[PROJECT_DESCRIPTION]': `Complete design process for ${project.name} including research, wireframes, prototypes, and final designs`,
      '[CLIENT_NAME]': project.name,
      '[ROLE]': 'Lead UX Designer',
      '[DURATION]': '3-6 months',
      '[TOOLS]': 'Sketch, Axure, Figma, Adobe Creative Suite',
      '[PROJECT_OVERVIEW]': `This comprehensive case study showcases the complete design process for ${project.name}, from initial research through final implementation.`,
      '[CHALLENGE_DESCRIPTION]': `Design and implement user-centered solutions that address complex business requirements while maintaining excellent user experience.`,
      '[SOLUTION_DESCRIPTION]': `Developed comprehensive design systems, interactive prototypes, and user flows that resulted in improved user engagement and business metrics.`
    };

    for (const [placeholder, value] of Object.entries(replacements)) {
      template = template.replace(new RegExp(placeholder, 'g'), value);
    }

    // Insert gallery HTML
    const galleryHTML = this.generateGalleryHTML(project, projectKey);
    template = template.replace('<!-- Gallery items will be populated here -->', galleryHTML);

    // Insert downloadables HTML
    const downloadablesHTML = this.generateDownloadablesHTML(projectKey);
    template = template.replace('<!-- Downloadable items will be populated here -->', downloadablesHTML);

    // Write the generated case study
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`Generated case study: ${outputPath}`);
  }

  // Main execution method
  async generateAll() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting gallery generation...');
    
    try {
      // Analyze and categorize images
      console.log('📊 Analyzing images...');
      const projects = await this.analyzeImages();
      
      // Generate thumbnails
      console.log('🖼️ Generating thumbnails...');
      await this.generateThumbnails(projects);
      
      // Generate case studies for each project
      console.log('📝 Generating case studies...');
      for (const [projectKey, project] of Object.entries(projects)) {
        if (project.images.length > 0) {
          await this.generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project);
        }
      }
      
      // Generate gallery configuration
      console.log('⚙️ Generating gallery configuration...');
      this.generateGalleryConfig(projects);
      
      console.log('✅ Gallery generation complete!');
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during gallery generation:', error);
    }
  }

  // Generate gallery configuration file
  generateGalleryConfig(projects) {
    const config = {
      projects: projects,
      generated: new Date().toISOString(),
      totalImages: Object.values(projects).reduce((sum, project) => sum + project.images.length, 0),
      categories: Object.values(projects).reduce((cats, project) => {
        project.images.forEach(img => {
          if (!cats.includes(img.category)) cats.push(img.category);
        });
        return cats;
      }, [])
    };

    const configPath = path.join(this.generatedDir, 'gallery-config.json');
    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
    console.log(`Gallery configuration saved: ${configPath}`);
  }
}

// Run the generator if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new GalleryGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = GalleryGenerator;
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.generatedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'generated');
    
    // Create generated directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.generatedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.generatedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();
      
      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);
      
      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>${project.name} - UX Case Study | Q10UX Portfolio</title>
  
  <!-- Bootstrap CSS -->
  <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
  
  <!-- Lightbox CSS -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/css/lightbox.min.css">
  
  <!-- Font Awesome -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css">
  
  <style>
    body {
      background: #0a0a0a;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
    }
    
    .gallery-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr));
      gap: 20px;
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }
    
    .gallery-item {
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
      border-radius: 8px;
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
      transition: transform 0.3s ease;
    }
    
    .gallery-item:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
    }
    
    .gallery-overlay {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      bottom: 0;
      background: rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.9);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
    }
    
    .gallery-item:hover .gallery-overlay {
      opacity: 1;
    }
    
    .gallery-caption {
      padding: 1rem;
      background: #1a1a1a;
    }
    
    .gallery-caption h4 {
      margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
    }
    
    .gallery-caption p {
      margin: 0;
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }
    
    .section-title {
      color: #ffffff;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    
    .lines {
      border: none;
      height: 2px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      margin: 2rem auto;
      width: 100px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="container-fluid py-5">
    <div class="row">
      <div class="col-12">
        <div class="text-center mb-5">
          <h1 class="display-4">${project.name}</h1>
          <p class="lead">UX Design Case Study</p>
          <hr class="lines">
        </div>
        
        ${this.generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project)}
        
        <div class="text-center mt-5">
          <a href="../index.html" class="btn btn-primary">
            <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Portfolio
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/js/lightbox.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>`;

    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-gallery.html`);
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`✅ Generated: ${projectKey}-gallery.html (${project.images.length} images)`);
  }

  // Generate main portfolio page
  generateMainPortfolio(projects) {
    let html = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>Q10UX Portfolio - UX Design Case Studies</title>
  
  <!-- Bootstrap CSS -->
  <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
  
  <!-- Font Awesome -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css">
  
  <style>
    body {
      background: #0a0a0a;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
    }
    
    .project-card {
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 2rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
      transition: transform 0.3s ease, box-shadow 0.3s ease;
      border: 1px solid #333;
    }
    
    .project-card:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
      border-color: #007bff;
    }
    
    .project-title {
      color: #ffffff;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    
    .project-count {
      color: #007bff;
      font-weight: bold;
    }
    
    .btn-primary {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      border: none;
      padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem;
      border-radius: 8px;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
    }
    
    .btn-primary:hover {
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.4);
    }
    
    .hero-section {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a1a 0%, #2d2d2d 100%);
      padding: 4rem 0;
      margin-bottom: 3rem;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="hero-section">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="text-center">
        <h1 class="display-3 mb-3">Q10UX Portfolio</h1>
        <p class="lead">Comprehensive UX Design Case Studies</p>
        <p class="text-muted">Showcasing work for major clients including AtmosFX, T-Mobile, Disney, and more</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="container">
    <div class="row">
`;

    Object.entries(projects).forEach(([key, project]) => {
      if (project.images.length > 0) {
        html += `
      <div class="col-lg-6 col-xl-4">
        <div class="project-card">
          <h3 class="project-title">${project.name}</h3>
          <p class="project-count">
            <i class="fas fa-images"></i> ${project.images.length} design deliverables
          </p>
          <p class="text-muted">
            Comprehensive case study showcasing the complete design process, from research to final implementation.
          </p>
          <a href="case-studies/${key}-gallery.html" class="btn btn-primary">
            <i class="fas fa-eye"></i> View Case Study
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
        `;
      }
    });

    html += `
    </div>
    
    <div class="text-center mt-5">
      <div class="row">
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <div class="text-center">
            <i class="fas fa-palette fa-3x text-primary mb-3"></i>
            <h4>Design Process</h4>
            <p class="text-muted">Complete UX methodology from research to implementation</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <div class="text-center">
            <i class="fas fa-users fa-3x text-primary mb-3"></i>
            <h4>User-Centered</h4>
            <p class="text-muted">Focus on user needs and business objectives</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <div class="text-center">
            <i class="fas fa-rocket fa-3x text-primary mb-3"></i>
            <h4>Results-Driven</h4>
            <p class="text-muted">Measurable improvements in user experience</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <footer class="bg-dark text-center py-4 mt-5">
    <div class="container">
      <p>&copy; 2025 Quentin Little. All rights reserved.</p>
      <p class="text-muted">UX Design Portfolio</p>
    </div>
  </footer>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>`;

    const outputPath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'index.html');
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, html);
    console.log(`✅ Generated: index.html`);
  }

  // Main execution method
  generateAll() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting simple gallery generation...');
    
    try {
      // Categorize images
      console.log('📊 Categorizing images...');
      const projects = this.categorizeImages();
      
      // Generate case studies for each project
      console.log('📝 Generating case studies...');
      Object.entries(projects).forEach(([key, project]) => {
        this.generateCaseStudy(key, project);
      });
      
      // Generate main portfolio page
      console.log('🏠 Generating main portfolio page...');
      this.generateMainPortfolio(projects);
      
      // Generate summary
      const totalImages = Object.values(projects).reduce((sum, project) => sum + project.images.length, 0);
      console.log(`\n✅ Gallery generation complete!`);
      console.log(`📊 Summary:`);
      console.log(`   - Total images: ${totalImages}`);
      console.log(`   - Projects: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length}`);
      console.log(`   - Generated files: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length + 1} HTML files`);
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during gallery generation:', error);
    }
  }
}

// Run the generator if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new SimpleGalleryGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = SimpleGalleryGenerator;
```
