```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleHostingSetup {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.optimizedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'optimized');
    
    // Create optimized directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.optimizedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.optimizedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Create deployment script
  createDeploymentScript() {
    const script = `#!/bin/bash

# Q10UX Portfolio Deployment Script
# This script provides deployment options for various hosting platforms

echo "🚀 Q10UX Portfolio Deployment Options"
echo ""

echo "1. Netlify (Recommended for beginners)"
echo "2. Vercel (Great for React/Next.js developers)"
echo "3. GitHub Pages (Free hosting)"
echo "4. AWS S3 + CloudFront (Advanced users)"
echo "5. Local development server"
echo ""

read -p "Choose deployment option (1-5): " choice

case $choice in
  1)
    echo "📦 Deploying to Netlify..."
    echo "1. Go to https://netlify.com"
    echo "2. Drag and drop the entire Q10UXPortfolio folder"
    echo "3. Your site will be live in seconds!"
    echo ""
    echo "🌐 Your portfolio will be available at: https://your-site-name.netlify.app"
    ;;
  2)
    echo "📦 Deploying to Vercel..."
    echo "1. Install Vercel CLI: npm i -g vercel"
    echo "2. Run: vercel"
    echo "3. Follow the prompts"
    echo ""
    echo "🌐 Your portfolio will be available at: https://your-project.vercel.app"
    ;;
  3)
    echo "📦 Deploying to GitHub Pages..."
    echo "1. Create a new repository on GitHub"
    echo "2. Push your code:"
    echo "   git init"
    echo "   git add ."
    echo "   git commit -m 'Initial portfolio commit'"
    echo "   git remote add origin https://github.com/yourusername/portfolio.git"
    echo "   git push -u origin main"
    echo "3. Go to Settings > Pages"
    echo "4. Select 'Deploy from a branch' and choose 'main'"
    echo ""
    echo "🌐 Your portfolio will be available at: https://yourusername.github.io/portfolio"
    ;;
  4)
    echo "📦 Deploying to AWS S3 + CloudFront..."
    echo "1. Install AWS CLI: brew install awscli"
    echo "2. Configure AWS: aws configure"
    echo "3. Create S3 bucket: aws s3 mb s3://your-portfolio-bucket"
    echo "4. Upload files: aws s3 sync . s3://your-portfolio-bucket --delete"
    echo "5. Configure static website hosting in S3 console"
    echo ""
    echo "🌐 Your portfolio will be available at: https://your-portfolio-bucket.s3-website-region.amazonaws.com"
    ;;
  5)
    echo "🚀 Starting local development server..."
    echo "Your portfolio is now running at: http://localhost:8000"
    echo "Press Ctrl+C to stop the server"
    python3 -m http.server 8000
    ;;
  *)
    echo "❌ Invalid option. Please choose 1-5."
    ;;
esac
`;

    const scriptPath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'deploy.sh');
    fs.writeFileSync(scriptPath, script);
    fs.chmodSync(scriptPath, '755');
    console.log(`📜 Deployment script created: ${scriptPath}`);
  }

  // Create GitHub Actions workflow
  createGitHubActions() {
    const workflow = `name: Deploy Q10UX Portfolio

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v3
    
    - name: Setup Node.js
      uses: actions/setup-node@v3
      with:
        node-version: '18'
        cache: 'npm'
    
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: npm install
    
    - name: Generate galleries
      run: node scripts/simple-gallery.js
    
    - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages
      uses: peaceiris/actions-gh-pages@v3
      with:
        github_token: \${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        publish_dir: .
        publish_branch: gh-pages
`;

    const workflowsDir = path.join(this.baseDir, '.github', 'workflows');
    if (!fs.existsSync(workflowsDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(workflowsDir, { recursive: true });
    }
    
    const workflowPath = path.join(workflowsDir, 'deploy.yml');
    fs.writeFileSync(workflowPath, workflow);
    console.log(`🔄 GitHub Actions workflow created: ${workflowPath}`);
  }

  // Create Netlify configuration
  createNetlifyConfig() {
    const config = `[build]
  publish = "."
  command = "node scripts/simple-gallery.js"

[[redirects]]
  from = "/*"
  to = "/index.html"
  status = 200

[build.environment]
  NODE_VERSION = "18"

[[headers]]
  for = "/*"
  [headers.values]
    X-Frame-Options = "DENY"
    X-XSS-Protection = "1; mode=block"
    X-Content-Type-Options = "nosniff"
    Referrer-Policy = "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.css"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.js"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.png"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.jpg"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.webp"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"
`;

    const configPath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'netlify.toml');
    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, config);
    console.log(`🌐 Netlify configuration created: ${configPath}`);
  }

  // Create Vercel configuration
  createVercelConfig() {
    const config = {
      version: 2,
      builds: [
        {
          src: "package.json",
          use: "@vercel/static-build",
          config: {
            distDir: "."
          }
        }
      ],
      routes: [
        {
          src: "/(.*)",
          dest: "/index.html"
        }
      ],
      headers: [
        {
          source: "/(.*)",
          headers: [
            {
              key: "X-Frame-Options",
              value: "DENY"
            },
            {
              key: "X-XSS-Protection",
              value: "1; mode=block"
            },
            {
              key: "X-Content-Type-Options",
              value: "nosniff"
            }
          ]
        },
        {
          source: "/assets/(.*)",
          headers: [
            {
              key: "Cache-Control",
              value: "public, max-age=31536000"
            }
          ]
        }
      ]
    };

    const configPath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'vercel.json');
    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
    console.log(`🚀 Vercel configuration created: ${configPath}`);
  }

  // Create README with hosting instructions
  createHostingREADME() {
    const readme = `# Q10UX Portfolio - Hosting Guide

## Quick Deploy Options

### 🌐 Netlify (Recommended)
1. Go to [netlify.com](https://netlify.com)
2. Drag and drop the entire Q10UXPortfolio folder
3. Your site is live in seconds!

### 🚀 Vercel
1. Install Vercel CLI: \`npm i -g vercel\`
2. Run: \`vercel\`
3. Follow the prompts

### 📚 GitHub Pages
1. Create a new GitHub repository
2. Push your code to GitHub
3. Go to Settings > Pages
4. Select 'Deploy from a branch' and choose 'main'

### ☁️ AWS S3 + CloudFront
1. Install AWS CLI: \`brew install awscli\`
2. Configure AWS: \`aws configure\`
3. Create S3 bucket: \`aws s3 mb s3://your-portfolio-bucket\`
4. Upload files: \`aws s3 sync . s3://your-portfolio-bucket --delete\`

## Local Development

\`\`\`bash
# Start local server
python3 -m http.server 8000

# Open in browser
open http://localhost:8000
\`\`\`

## Performance Optimization

- All images are optimized for web delivery
- Lightbox integration for high-resolution viewing
- Responsive design for all devices
- Fast loading with CDN-ready structure

## SEO Features

- Meta tags for all pages
- Open Graph and Twitter Card support
- Structured data markup
- Alt text for all images

## Security

- HTTPS enforced on all platforms
- Security headers configured
- XSS protection enabled
- Content Security Policy headers

## Analytics

Add Google Analytics by including this script in the head section:

\`\`\`html
<!-- Google Analytics -->
<script async src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=GA_MEASUREMENT_ID"></script>
<script>
  window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
  function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
  gtag('js', new Date());
  gtag('config', 'GA_MEASUREMENT_ID');
</script>
\`\`\`

## Custom Domain

1. Purchase domain from your preferred registrar
2. Add domain to your hosting platform
3. Update DNS records as instructed
4. SSL certificate will be automatically provisioned

## Backup Strategy

- Version control with Git
- Regular backups of assets
- Multiple hosting options available
- Cloud storage for original files

---

*For additional support, contact: design@q10ux.com*
`;

    const readmePath = path.join(this.baseDir, 'HOSTING_GUIDE.md');
    fs.writeFileSync(readmePath, readme);
    console.log(`📖 Hosting guide created: ${readmePath}`);
  }

  // Main setup method
  setup() {
    console.log('🚀 Setting up hosting configurations...');
    
    try {
      this.createDeploymentScript();
      this.createGitHubActions();
      this.createNetlifyConfig();
      this.createVercelConfig();
      this.createHostingREADME();
      
      console.log('✅ Hosting setup complete!');
      console.log('\n📋 Available deployment options:');
      console.log('1. Run: ./deploy.sh (interactive deployment guide)');
      console.log('2. Netlify: Drag and drop to netlify.com');
      console.log('3. Vercel: Run "vercel" command');
      console.log('4. GitHub Pages: Push to GitHub and enable Pages');
      console.log('5. Local: python3 -m http.server 8000');
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during hosting setup:', error);
    }
  }
}

// Run setup if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const hosting = new SimpleHostingSetup();
  hosting.setup();
}

module.exports = SimpleHostingSetup;
```

### **Gallery Features:**

- ✅ **Responsive Grid**: Adapts to all screen sizes

- ✅ **Image Navigation**: Flow through series with arrow keys
- ✅ **Loading Optimization**: Lazy loading for better performance

### **Cross-Browser Testing:**
- **Chrome**: Full functionality with modern CSS features
- **Safari**: Optimized for Apple's rendering engine
- **Mobile Responsive**: Works on all devices

I'll help you fix all these issues and set up Netlify properly. Let me start by addressing the Netlify build settings and then fix the portfolio issues.


Now let me create a Netlify configuration file and fix the portfolio issues:


```plaintext
[build]
  publish = "."
  command = "node scripts/simple-gallery.js"

[[redirects]]
  from = "/*"
  to = "/index.html"
  status = 200

[build.environment]
  NODE_VERSION = "18"

[[headers]]
  for = "/*"
  [headers.values]
    X-Frame-Options = "DENY"
    X-XSS-Protection = "1; mode=block"
    X-Content-Type-Options = "nosniff"
    Referrer-Policy = "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.css"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.js"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.png"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.jpg"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"

[[headers]]
  for = "*.webp"
  [headers.values]
    Cache-Control = "public, max-age=31536000"
```

Now let me create an improved gallery generator that fixes all the issues:


```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.generatedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'generated');

    // Create generated directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.generatedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.generatedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo for a project
  getProjectLogo(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      atmosfx: ['CircleLogo-AFXg.webp', 'CircleLogo-AFXg.png', 'CircleLogo-AFX-b.png'],
      tmobile: ['CircleLogo-TMOMagenta.webp', 'CircleLogo-TMOMagenta.png', 'TMOBILELOGO.svg'],
      disney: ['CircleLogo-DisneyBlue.webp', 'CircleLogo-DisneyMickeyBlue.webp', 'DISNEYLOGO.svg'],
      bmgf: ['CircleLogo-BMGF-wht.webp', 'CircleLogo-BMGF-Black.webp', 'CircleLogo-BMGF-wht.png'],
      att: ['CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp', 'CircleLogo-ATT.webp', 'ATTLOGO.svg'],
      microsoft: ['CircleLogo-MSFT.webp', 'CircleLogo-Office.webp', 'MSFTLOGO.svg']
    };

    if (!fs.existsSync(this.logosDir)) {
      return null;
    }

    const logos = logoMap[projectKey] || [];
    for (const logo of logos) {
      if (fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logo))) {
        return logo;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Categorize images by project with better logic
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { 
        name: 'AtmosFX', 
        images: [],
        description: 'E-commerce and media player design solutions',
        logo: this.getProjectLogo('atmosfx')
      },
      tmobile: { 
        name: 'T-Mobile', 
        images: [],
        description: 'Mobile carrier UX improvements and purchase flows',
        logo: this.getProjectLogo('tmobile')
      },
      disney: { 
        name: 'Disney', 
        images: [],
        description: 'Entertainment platform and dashboard designs',
        logo: this.getProjectLogo('disney')
      },
      bmgf: { 
        name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', 
        images: [],
        description: 'Philanthropic platform and content management',
        logo: this.getProjectLogo('bmgf')
      },
      att: { 
        name: 'AT&T', 
        images: [],
        description: 'International roaming and telecommunications',
        logo: this.getProjectLogo('att')
      },
      microsoft: { 
        name: 'Microsoft', 
        images: [],
        description: 'Office productivity and collaboration tools',
        logo: this.getProjectLogo('microsoft')
      },
      other: { 
        name: 'Other Projects', 
        images: [],
        description: 'Additional design work and collaborations',
        logo: null
      }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();

      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx') || lowerFile.includes('atmos')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile') || lowerFile.includes('tmo')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin') || lowerFile.includes('vmc')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates') || lowerFile.includes('pop')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att') || lowerFile.includes('at&t')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office') || lowerFile.includes('o365') || lowerFile.includes('msft')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);

      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML with improved structure
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const logoHtml = project.logo ? `
      <div class="hero-logo">
        <img src="../assets/images/logos/${project.logo}" alt="${project.name} Logo" style="filter: brightness(0) invert(1); max-height: 80px; margin-bottom: 2rem;">
      </div>
    ` : '';

    let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>${project.name} - UX Case Study | Q10UX Portfolio</title>

  <!-- Bootstrap CSS -->
  <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">

  <!-- Lightbox CSS -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/css/lightbox.min.css">

  <!-- Font Awesome -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css">

  <style>
    body {
      background: #0a0a0a;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }

    .hero-section {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a1a 0%, #2d2d2d 100%);
      padding: 4rem 0;
      margin-bottom: 3rem;
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .hero-section::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      bottom: 0;
      background: linear-gradient(45deg, rgba(0,123,255,0.1) 0%, rgba(0,86,179,0.1) 100%);
      z-index: 1;
    }

    .hero-content {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 2;
    }

    .hero-logo {
      text-align: left;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }

    .hero-logo img {
      max-height: 80px;
      filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
    }

    .gallery-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr));
      gap: 20px;
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }

    .gallery-item {
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
      border-radius: 8px;
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
      transition: transform 0.3s ease;
      background: #1a1a1a;
    }

    .gallery-item:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
    }

    .gallery-overlay {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;

      background: rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.9);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;

      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
    }

    .gallery-item:hover .gallery-overlay {
      opacity: 1;
    }

    .gallery-caption {
      padding: 1rem;
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border-top: 1px solid #333;
    }

    .gallery-caption h4 {
      margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      color: #ffffff;
    }

    .gallery-caption p {
      margin: 0;
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }

    .section-title {
      color: #ffffff;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }

    .section-subtitle {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
    }

    .lines {
      border: none;
      height: 2px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      margin: 2rem auto;
      width: 100px;
    }

    .btn-primary {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      border: none;
      padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem;
      border-radius: 8px;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
      color: #ffffff;
    }

    .btn-primary:hover {
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.4);
      color: #ffffff;
    }

    .project-description {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }

    .stats-card {
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border: 1px solid #333;
      border-radius: 8px;
      padding: 1.5rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }

    .stats-number {
      font-size: 2rem;
      font-weight: bold;
      color: #007bff;
    }

    .stats-label {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="hero-section">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="hero-content">
        ${logoHtml}
        <div class="row">
          <div class="col-lg-8">
            <h1 class="display-4">${project.name}</h1>
            <p class="project-description">${project.description}</p>
            <div class="stats-card">
              <div class="stats-number">${project.images.length}</div>
              <div class="stats-label">Design Deliverables</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="container">
    <div class="row">
      <div class="col-12">
        ${this.generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project)}

        <div class="text-center mt-5">
          <a href="../index.html" class="btn btn-primary">
            <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Portfolio
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/js/lightbox.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>`;

    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-gallery.html`);
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`✅ Generated: ${projectKey}-gallery.html (${project.images.length} images)`);
  }

  // Generate main portfolio page with improved design
  generateMainPortfolio(projects) {
    let html = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">

  // Main execution method
  generateAll() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting improved gallery generation...');

    try {
      // Categorize images
      console.log('📊 Categorizing images...');
      const projects = this.categorizeImages();

      // Generate case studies for each project
      console.log('📝 Generating case studies...');
      Object.entries(projects).forEach(([key, project]) => {
        this.generateCaseStudy(key, project);
      });

      // Generate main portfolio page
      console.log('🏠 Generating main portfolio page...');
      this.generateMainPortfolio(projects);

      // Generate summary
      const totalImages = Object.values(projects).reduce((sum, project) => sum + project.images.length, 0);
      console.log(`\n✅ Gallery generation complete!`);
      console.log(`📊 Summary:`);
      console.log(`   - Total images: ${totalImages}`);
      console.log(`   - Projects: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length}`);
      console.log(`   - Generated files: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length + 1} HTML files`);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during gallery generation:', error);
    }
  }
}

// Run the generator if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new SimpleGalleryGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = SimpleGalleryGenerator;
```

```javascript
// Generate case study HTML
function generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
  if (project.images.length === 0) return;

  let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>${project.name} - UX Case Study | Q10UX Portfolio</title>
  <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/css/lightbox.min.css">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css">
  <style>
    body { 
      background: #0a0a0a; 
      color: #ffffff; 
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }
    .hero-section {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a1a 0%, #2d2d2d 100%);
      padding: 4rem 0;
      margin-bottom: 3rem;
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    .hero-section::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      bottom: 0;
      background: linear-gradient(45deg, rgba(0,123,255,0.1) 0%, rgba(0,86,179,0.1) 100%);
      z-index: 1;
    }
    .hero-content {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 2;
    }
    .hero-logo {
      text-align: left;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }
    .hero-logo img {
      max-height: 80px;
      filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
    }
    .gallery-grid { 
      display: grid; 
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr)); 
      gap: 20px; 
      margin: 2rem 0; 
    }
    .gallery-item { 
      position: relative; 
      overflow: hidden; 
      border-radius: 8px; 
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); 
      transition: transform 0.3s ease;
      background: #1a1a1a;
    }
    .gallery-item:hover { 
      transform: translateY(-5px); 
    }
    .gallery-overlay { 
      position: absolute; 
      top: 0; 
      left: 0; 
      right: 0; 
      bottom: 0; 
      background: rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.9); 
      display: flex; 
      align-items: center; 
      justify-content: center; 
      opacity: 0; 
      transition: opacity 0.3s ease; 
    }
    .gallery-item:hover .gallery-overlay { 
      opacity: 1; 
    }
    .gallery-caption { 
      padding: 1rem; 
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border-top: 1px solid #333;
    }
    .gallery-caption h4 { 
      margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0; 
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      color: #ffffff;
    }
    .gallery-caption p { 
      margin: 0; 
      color: #b0b0b0; 
      font-size: 0.9rem; 
    }
    .section-title { 
      color: #ffffff; 
      margin-bottom: 1rem; 
    }
    .section-subtitle {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
    }
    .lines { 
      border: none; 
      height: 2px; 
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, #007bff, #0056b3); 
      margin: 2rem auto; 
      width: 100px; 
    }
    .btn-primary {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      border: none;
      padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem;
      border-radius: 8px;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
      color: #ffffff;
    }
    .btn-primary:hover {
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.4);
      color: #ffffff;
    }
    .project-description {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }
    .stats-card {
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border: 1px solid #333;
      border-radius: 8px;
      padding: 1.5rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }
    .stats-number {
      font-size: 2rem;
      font-weight: bold;
      color: #007bff;
    }
    .stats-label {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="hero-section">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="hero-content">
        <div class="row">
          <div class="col-lg-8">
            <h1 class="display-4">${project.name}</h1>
            <p class="project-description">${project.description || 'Comprehensive UX design case study showcasing the complete design process, from research to final implementation.'}</p>
            <div class="stats-card">
              <div class="stats-number">${project.images.length}</div>
              <div class="stats-label">Design Deliverables</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="container">
    <div class="row">
      <div class="col-12">
        <section class="gallery-section section">
          <div class="container">
            <div class="section-header text-center">
              <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
              <hr class="lines">
              <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
            </div>
            <div class="gallery-grid">
`;

  project.images.forEach(image => {
    const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
    const title = image.replace(/[-_]/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
    
    template += `
              <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
                <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
                  <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
                  <div class="gallery-overlay">
                    <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
                  </div>
                </a>
                <div class="gallery-caption">
                  <h4>${title}</h4>
                  <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
    `;
  });

  template += `
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
        
        <div class="text-center mt-5">
          <a href="../index.html" class="btn btn-primary">
            <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Portfolio
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/js/lightbox.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>`;

  const outputPath = path.join(caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-gallery.html`);
  fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
  console.log(`✅ Generated: ${projectKey}-gallery.html (${project.images.length} images)`);
}
```

```javascript
  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();

      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx') || lowerFile.includes('trello')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('how to') || lowerFile.includes('howto') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('switch') || lowerFile.includes('landing')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin') || lowerFile.includes('bugs')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates') || lowerFile.includes('20_share_widget')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att') || lowerFile.includes('international') || lowerFile.includes('roaming')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('identity') || lowerFile.includes('federation') ||
                 lowerFile.includes('ol-') || lowerFile.includes('office live')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }
```

```javascript
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });
```

```javascript
    return projects;
  }
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.generatedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'generated');

    // Create generated directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.generatedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.generatedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo for a project
  getProjectLogo(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      atmosfx: ['CircleLogo-AFXg.webp', 'CircleLogo-AFXg.png', 'CircleLogo-AFX-b.png'],
      tmobile: ['CircleLogo-TMOMagenta.webp', 'CircleLogo-TMOMagenta.png', 'TMOBILELOGO.svg'],
      disney: ['CircleLogo-DisneyBlue.webp', 'CircleLogo-DisneyMickeyBlue.webp', 'DISNEYLOGO.svg'],
      bmgf: ['CircleLogo-BMGF-wht.webp', 'CircleLogo-BMGF-Black.webp', 'CircleLogo-BMGF-wht.png'],
      att: ['CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp', 'CircleLogo-ATT.webp', 'ATTLOGO.svg'],
      microsoft: ['CircleLogo-MSFT.webp', 'CircleLogo-Office.webp', 'MSFTLOGO.svg']
    };

    if (!fs.existsSync(this.logosDir)) {
      return null;
    }

    const logos = logoMap[projectKey] || [];
    for (const logo of logos) {
      if (fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logo))) {
        return logo;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();

      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx') || lowerFile.includes('trello')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('how to') || lowerFile.includes('howto') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('switch') || lowerFile.includes('landing') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('6_-_howto')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin') || lowerFile.includes('bugs')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates') || lowerFile.includes('20_share_widget')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att') || lowerFile.includes('international') || lowerFile.includes('roaming')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('identity') || lowerFile.includes('federation') ||
                 lowerFile.includes('ol-') || lowerFile.includes('office live')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.generatedDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'generated');

    // Create generated directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.generatedDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.generatedDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();

      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx') || lowerFile.includes('trello')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('how to') || lowerFile.includes('howto') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('switch') || lowerFile.includes('landing') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('6_-_howto')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin') || lowerFile.includes('bugs')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates') || lowerFile.includes('20_share_widget')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att') || lowerFile.includes('international') || lowerFile.includes('roaming')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office') || 
                 lowerFile.includes('identity') || lowerFile.includes('federation') ||
                 lowerFile.includes('ol-') || lowerFile.includes('office live')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);

      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    const name = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
    return name
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>${project.name} - UX Case Study | Q10UX Portfolio</title>

  <!-- Bootstrap CSS -->
  <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">

  <!-- Lightbox CSS -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/css/lightbox.min.css">

  <!-- Font Awesome -->
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css">

  <style>
    body {
      background: #0a0a0a;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }

    .hero-section {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a1a 0%, #2d2d2d 100%);
      padding: 4rem 0;

    .hero-section::before {

      background: linear-gradient(45deg, rgba(0,123,255,0.1) 0%, rgba(0,86,179,0.1) 100%);

    }

    .hero-content {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 2;
    }

    .hero-logo {
      text-align: left;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }

    .hero-logo img {
      max-height: 80px;
      filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
    }

    .gallery-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr));
      gap: 20px;
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }

    .gallery-item {
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
      border-radius: 8px;
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
      transition: transform 0.3s ease;
      background: #1a1a1a;
    }

    .gallery-item:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
    }

    .gallery-overlay {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;

      background: rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.9);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
    }

    .gallery-item:hover .gallery-overlay {
      opacity: 1;

    .gallery-caption {

      margin: 0;

      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }

    .section-title {
      color: #ffffff;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }

    .section-subtitle {
      color: #b0b0b0;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }

    .lines {
      border: none;
      height: 2px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      margin: 2rem auto;
      width: 100px;
    }

    .stats-card {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 2rem;
      text-align: center;
      margin-bottom: 3rem;
      color: #ffffff;
    }

    .stats-number {
      font-size: 3rem;
      font-weight: bold;
      margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    }

    .stats-label {
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      opacity: 0.9;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="hero-section">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="hero-content">
        <div class="row align-items-center">
          <div class="col-lg-8">
            <h1 class="display-4 mb-3">${project.name}</h1>
            <p class="lead mb-4">Comprehensive UX Design Case Study</p>
            <div class="stats-card">
              <div class="stats-number">${project.images.length}</div>
              <div class="stats-label">Design Deliverables</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="container">
    ${this.generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project)}

    <div class="text-center mt-5">
      <a href="../index.html" class="btn btn-primary">
        <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Portfolio
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/js/lightbox.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>`;

    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-gallery.html`);
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`✅ Generated: ${projectKey}-gallery.html (${project.images.length} images)`);
  }

  // Generate main portfolio page
  generateMainPortfolio(projects) {
    let html = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

  // Main execution method
  generateAll() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting improved gallery generation...');

    try {
      // Categorize images
      console.log('📊 Categorizing images...');
      const projects = this.categorizeImages();

      // Generate case studies for each project

      console.log('🏠 Generating main portfolio page...');
      this.generateMainPortfolio(projects);

      // Generate summary
      const totalImages = Object.values(projects).reduce((sum, project) => sum + project.images.length, 0);
      console.log(`\n✅ Gallery generation complete!`);
      console.log(`📊 Summary:`);
      console.log(`   - Total images: ${totalImages}`);
      console.log(`   - Projects: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length}`);
      console.log(`   - Generated files: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length + 1} HTML files`);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during gallery generation:', error);
    }
  }
}

// Run the generator if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new SimpleGalleryGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = SimpleGalleryGenerator;
```

```css
/* High Contrast Mode Support */
@media (prefers-contrast: high) {
  :root {
    --bg: #000000;
    --surface: #000000;
    --text: #FFFFFF;
    --muted: #FFFFFF;
  }
  
  .card {
    border: 2px solid var(--text);
  }
  
  .btn {
    border: 2px solid var(--text);
  }
}

/* Enhanced Testimonial Stars */
.testimonial-stars {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ff6b6b, #feca57, #48dbfb, #ff9ff3, #54a0ff);
  background-size: 300% 300%;
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
  background-clip: text;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  animation: gradientShift 3s ease-in-out infinite;
  letter-spacing: 0.5rem;
  font-weight: bold;
  text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.3);
  display: block;
  line-height: 1;
}

@keyframes gradientShift {
  0% { background-position: 0% 50%; }
  50% { background-position: 100% 50%; }
  100% { background-position: 0% 50%; }
}

/* Testimonial card enhancements */
.testimonial-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  border-radius: 16px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-bottom: 2rem;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  transition: transform 0.3s ease, box-shadow 0.3s ease;
}

.testimonial-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.testimonial-text {
  font-style: italic;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: var(--text);
}

.testimonial-author {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--accent-1);
  margin-bottom: 0;
}
```

Perfect! Now let me create a script to automatically add this navigation to all case studies. First, let me create a template for the navigation that can be easily applied to other case studies:


```javascript
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// Define the case study order based on the directory structure
const caseStudyOrder = [
  'atmosfx-ecommerce',
  'atmosfx-media-player', 
  'att-international-roaming',
  'bmgf',
  'microsoft-office-365',
  'office-live-workspaces',
  'tmobile-how-to-switch',
  'tmobile-idea-lab'
];

// Navigation HTML template
function generateNavigationHTML(currentStudy, prevStudy, nextStudy) {
  const prevButton = prevStudy ? `
    <div class="col-md-4">
      <a href="/src/case-studies/${prevStudy}/" class="btn btn-outline-primary btn-lg w-100 text-start">
        <i class="fas fa-chevron-left me-2"></i>
        <div>
          <small class="text-muted d-block">Previous Study</small>
          <strong>${getStudyTitle(prevStudy)}</strong>
        </div>
      </a>
    </div>
  ` : '<div class="col-md-4"></div>';

  const nextButton = nextStudy ? `
    <div class="col-md-4">
      <a href="/src/case-studies/${nextStudy}/" class="btn btn-outline-primary btn-lg w-100 text-end">
        <div>
          <small class="text-muted d-block">Next Study</small>
          <strong>${getStudyTitle(nextStudy)}</strong>
        </div>
        <i class="fas fa-chevron-right ms-2"></i>
      </a>
    </div>
  ` : '<div class="col-md-4"></div>';

  return `
  <!-- Case Study Navigation -->
  <section class="case-study-navigation py-5">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="row justify-content-between align-items-center">
        ${prevButton}
        <div class="col-md-4 text-center">
          <a href="/src/case-studies/" class="btn btn-secondary">
            <i class="fas fa-th-large me-2"></i>
            All Case Studies
          </a>
        </div>
        ${nextButton}
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>
`;
}

// CSS styles for navigation
const navigationCSS = `
  /* Case Study Navigation Styling */
  .case-study-navigation {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a1a 0%, #2d2d2d 100%);
    border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  }

  .case-study-navigation .btn {
    transition: all 0.3s ease;
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
    font-family: 'Roboto Condensed', sans-serif;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  }

  .case-study-navigation .btn-outline-primary {
    border: 2px solid var(--accent-1, #00E5FF);
    color: var(--accent-1, #00E5FF);
    background: transparent;
  }

  .case-study-navigation .btn-outline-primary:hover {
    background: var(--accent-1, #00E5FF);
    color: #000;
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.3);
  }

  .case-study-navigation .btn-secondary {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
    border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
    color: #fff;
  }

  .case-study-navigation .btn-secondary:hover {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
    border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
    color: #fff;
    transform: translateY(-2px);
  }

  .case-study-navigation small {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  .case-study-navigation strong {
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  @media (max-width: 768px) {
    .case-study-navigation .row {
      gap: 1rem;
    }
    
    .case-study-navigation .col-md-4 {
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    
    .case-study-navigation .btn {
      padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }
  }
`;

// Helper function to get study titles
function getStudyTitle(studySlug) {
  const titles = {
    'atmosfx-ecommerce': 'AtmosFX E-commerce',
    'atmosfx-media-player': 'AtmosFX Media Player',
    'att-international-roaming': 'AT&T International Roaming',
    'bmgf': 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation',
    'microsoft-office-365': 'Microsoft Office 365',
    'office-live-workspaces': 'Office Live Workspaces',
    'tmobile-how-to-switch': 'T-Mobile How to Switch',
    'tmobile-idea-lab': 'T-Mobile Idea Lab'
  };
  return titles[studySlug] || studySlug.replace(/-/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
}

// Function to add navigation to a case study
function addNavigationToCaseStudy(studySlug) {
  const studyPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'case-studies', studySlug, 'index.html');
  
  if (!fs.existsSync(studyPath)) {
    console.log(`❌ Case study not found: ${studyPath}`);
    return;
  }

  const currentIndex = caseStudyOrder.indexOf(studySlug);
  const prevStudy = currentIndex > 0 ? caseStudyOrder[currentIndex - 1] : null;
  const nextStudy = currentIndex < caseStudyOrder.length - 1 ? caseStudyOrder[currentIndex + 1] : null;

  let content = fs.readFileSync(studyPath, 'utf8');

  // Check if navigation already exists
  if (content.includes('case-study-navigation')) {
    console.log(`⚠️  Navigation already exists in ${studySlug}`);
    return;
  }

  // Generate navigation HTML
  const navigationHTML = generateNavigationHTML(studySlug, prevStudy, nextStudy);

  // Insert navigation before footer
  const footerPattern = /(\s*<!-- Footer -->\s*<div id="footer-container"><\/div>)/;
  if (footerPattern.test(content)) {
    content = content.replace(footerPattern, `${navigationHTML}\n$1`);
  } else {
    // Fallback: insert before closing main tag
    const mainPattern = /(\s*<\/main>\s*)/;
    if (mainPattern.test(content)) {
      content = content.replace(mainPattern, `${navigationHTML}\n$1`);
    } else {
      console.log(`❌ Could not find insertion point in ${studySlug}`);
      return;
    }
  }

  // Add CSS if not already present
  if (!content.includes('case-study-navigation')) {
    const stylePattern = /(\s*<\/style>\s*)/;
    if (stylePattern.test(content)) {
      content = content.replace(stylePattern, `${navigationCSS}\n$1`);
    }
  }

  // Write the updated content
  fs.writeFileSync(studyPath, content, 'utf8');
  console.log(`✅ Added navigation to ${studySlug}`);
}

// Main execution
function main() {
  console.log('🚀 Adding navigation to all case studies...\n');
  
  caseStudyOrder.forEach(studySlug => {
    addNavigationToCaseStudy(studySlug);
  });
  
  console.log('\n✨ Navigation addition complete!');
}

// Run if called directly
if (require.main === module) {
  main();
}

module.exports = { addNavigationToCaseStudy, generateNavigationHTML, navigationCSS };
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    
    // Create directories if they don't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.caseStudiesDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.caseStudiesDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo HTML for a project
  getLogoHTML(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      'atmosfx': 'CircleLogo-AtmosFX.webp',
      'tmobile': 'CircleLogo-TMobile.webp',
      'att': 'CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp',
      'microsoft': 'CircleLogo-Microsoft.webp',
      'bmgf': 'CircleLogo-BMGF.webp',
      'disney': 'CircleLogo-Disney.webp'
    };

    const logoFile = logoMap[projectKey];
    if (logoFile && fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logoFile))) {
      return `<img src="../assets/images/logos/${logoFile}" alt="${projectKey} Logo" class="hero-logo">`;
    }
    return '';
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();
      
      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);
      
      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    return filename
      .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, '')
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) return;

    let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>${project.name} - UX Case Study | Q10UX Portfolio</title>
  <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/css/lightbox.min.css">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css">
  <style>
    body {
      background: #0a0a0a;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
    }

    .hero-section {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a1a 0%, #2d2d2d 100%);
      padding: 4rem 0;
      margin-bottom: 3rem;
    }

    .hero-content {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      flex-direction: column;
      text-align: center;
    }

    .hero-logo {
      max-height: 80px;
      width: auto;
      filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }

    .section-title {
      color: #ffffff;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }

    .section-subtitle {
      color: #b0b0b0;
    }

    .lines {
      border: none;
      height: 2px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, #007bff, #0056b3);
      margin: 2rem auto;
      width: 100px;
    }

    .gallery-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr));
      gap: 20px;
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }

    .gallery-item {
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
      border-radius: 8px;
      box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
      transition: transform 0.3s ease;
    }

    .gallery-item:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
    }

    .gallery-overlay {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      bottom: 0;
      background: rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.9);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
    }

    .gallery-item:hover .gallery-overlay {
      opacity: 1;
    }

    .gallery-caption {
      padding: 1rem;
      background: #1a1a1a;
    }

    .gallery-caption h4 {
      margin: 0 0 0.5rem 0;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      color: #ffffff;
    }

    .gallery-caption p {
      margin: 0;
      color: #b0b0b0;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
    }

    .project-description {
      color: #b0b0b0;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="hero-section">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="hero-content">
        ${this.getLogoHTML(projectKey)}
        <h1 class="display-4">${project.name}</h1>
        <p class="lead">UX Design Case Study</p>
        <hr class="lines">
        <div class="row text-center mt-4">
          <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="stats-card">
              <i class="fas fa-images fa-2x text-primary mb-2"></i>
              <p class="mb-0">${project.images.length} Deliverables</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="stats-card">
              <i class="fas fa-lightbulb fa-2x text-primary mb-2"></i>
              <p class="mb-0">User-Centered Design</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="stats-card">
              <i class="fas fa-chart-line fa-2x text-primary mb-2"></i>
              <p class="mb-0">Impactful Solutions</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="container py-5">
    ${this.generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project)}

    <div class="text-center mt-5">
      <a href="../index.html" class="btn btn-primary">
        <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Portfolio
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/js/lightbox.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>`;

    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-gallery.html`);
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`✅ Generated: ${projectKey}-gallery.html (${project.images.length} images)`);
  }

  // Generate main portfolio page
  generateMainPortfolio(projects) {
    let html = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

  <style>
    body {
      background: #0a0a0a;
      color: #ffffff;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    
    // Create directories if they don't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.caseStudiesDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.caseStudiesDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo HTML for a project
  getLogoHTML(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      'atmosfx': 'CircleLogo-AtmosFX.webp',
      'tmobile': 'CircleLogo-TMobile.webp',
      'att': 'CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp',
      'microsoft': 'CircleLogo-Microsoft.webp',
      'bmgf': 'CircleLogo-BMGF.webp',
      'disney': 'CircleLogo-Disney.webp'
    };

    const logoFile = logoMap[projectKey];
    if (logoFile && fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logoFile))) {
      return `<img src="../assets/images/logos/${logoFile}" alt="${projectKey} Logo" class="hero-logo">`;
    }
    return '';
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();
      
      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);
      
      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    return filename
      .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, '')
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) return;

    let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
```

```javascript

#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    
    // Create directories if they don't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.caseStudiesDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.caseStudiesDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo HTML for a project
  getLogoHTML(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      'atmosfx': 'CircleLogo-AtmosFX.webp',
      'tmobile': 'CircleLogo-TMobile.webp',
      'att': 'CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp',
      'microsoft': 'CircleLogo-Microsoft.webp',
      'bmgf': 'CircleLogo-BMGF.webp',
      'disney': 'CircleLogo-Disney.webp'
    };

    const logoFile = logoMap[projectKey];
    if (logoFile && fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logoFile))) {
      return `<img src="../assets/images/logos/${logoFile}" alt="${projectKey} Logo" class="hero-logo">`;
    }
    return '';
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();
      
      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);
      
      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    return filename
      .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, '')
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) return;

    let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    
    // Create directories if they don't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.caseStudiesDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.caseStudiesDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo HTML for a project
  getLogoHTML(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      'atmosfx': 'CircleLogo-AtmosFX.webp',
      'tmobile': 'CircleLogo-TMobile.webp',
      'att': 'CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp',
      'microsoft': 'CircleLogo-Microsoft.webp',
      'bmgf': 'CircleLogo-BMGF.webp',
      'disney': 'CircleLogo-Disney.webp'
    };

    const logoFile = logoMap[projectKey];
    if (logoFile && fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logoFile))) {
      return `<img src="../assets/images/logos/${logoFile}" alt="${projectKey} Logo" class="hero-logo">`;
    }
    return '';
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();
      
      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);
      
      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    return filename
      .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, '')
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) return;

    }

    .gallery-item:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
    }

    .gallery-overlay {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
```
