Let me try a different approach and delete the file first, then recreate it:


```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class SimpleGalleryGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.imagesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images');
    this.caseStudiesDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'case-studies');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    
    // Create directories if they don't exist
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.caseStudiesDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.caseStudiesDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  // Get all image files
  getImageFiles() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.imagesDir)) {
      console.log('Images directory not found');
      return [];
    }

    const files = fs.readdirSync(this.imagesDir);
    return files.filter(file => {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      return ['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp'].includes(ext);
    });
  }

  // Get logo HTML for a project
  getLogoHTML(projectKey) {
    const logoMap = {
      'atmosfx': 'CircleLogo-AtmosFX.webp',
      'tmobile': 'CircleLogo-TMobile.webp',
      'att': 'CircleLogo-ATTBlue.webp',
      'microsoft': 'CircleLogo-Microsoft.webp',
      'bmgf': 'CircleLogo-BMGF.webp',
      'disney': 'CircleLogo-Disney.webp'
    };

    const logoFile = logoMap[projectKey];
    if (logoFile && fs.existsSync(path.join(this.logosDir, logoFile))) {
      return `<img src="../assets/images/logos/${logoFile}" alt="${projectKey} Logo" class="hero-logo">`;
    }
    return '';
  }

  // Categorize images by project
  categorizeImages() {
    const imageFiles = this.getImageFiles();
    const projects = {
      atmosfx: { name: 'AtmosFX', images: [] },
      tmobile: { name: 'T-Mobile', images: [] },
      disney: { name: 'Disney', images: [] },
      bmgf: { name: 'Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation', images: [] },
      att: { name: 'AT&T', images: [] },
      microsoft: { name: 'Microsoft', images: [] },
      other: { name: 'Other Projects', images: [] }
    };

    imageFiles.forEach(file => {
      const lowerFile = file.toLowerCase();
      
      if (lowerFile.includes('atmosfx') || lowerFile.includes('afx')) {
        projects.atmosfx.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('tmobile') || lowerFile.includes('t-mobile')) {
        projects.tmobile.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('disney') || lowerFile.includes('club-penguin')) {
        projects.disney.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('bmgf') || lowerFile.includes('gates')) {
        projects.bmgf.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('att')) {
        projects.att.images.push(file);
      } else if (lowerFile.includes('microsoft') || lowerFile.includes('office')) {
        projects.microsoft.images.push(file);
      } else {
        projects.other.images.push(file);
      }
    });

    return projects;
  }

  // Generate gallery HTML for a project
  generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) {
      return '';
    }

    let html = `
    <!-- Gallery Section for ${project.name} -->
    <section class="gallery-section section">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="section-header text-center">
          <h2 class="section-title">${project.name} - Design Deliverables</h2>
          <hr class="lines">
          <p class="section-subtitle">Explore the complete design process through wireframes, prototypes, and final designs</p>
        </div>
        <div class="gallery-grid">
    `;

    project.images.forEach(image => {
      const imagePath = `../assets/images/${image}`;
      const title = this.getImageTitle(image);
      
      html += `
        <div class="gallery-item fade-in">
          <a href="${imagePath}" data-lightbox="${projectKey}-gallery" data-title="${title}">
            <img src="${imagePath}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 250px; object-fit: cover;">
            <div class="gallery-overlay">
              <i class="fas fa-expand"></i>
            </div>
          </a>
          <div class="gallery-caption">
            <h4>${title}</h4>
            <p>Design deliverable for ${project.name}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    `;

    return html;
  }

  // Get image title from filename
  getImageTitle(filename) {
    return filename
      .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, '')
      .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, l => l.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Generate case study HTML
  generateCaseStudy(projectKey, project) {
    if (project.images.length === 0) return;

    let template = `<!DOCTYPE html>

    /* Ensure dark text on light backgrounds */

  generateAll() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting simple gallery generation...');

      // Categorize images

      // Generate case studies for each project

      Object.entries(projects).forEach(([key, project]) => {
        this.generateCaseStudy(key, project);
      });
      
      // Generate main portfolio page
      console.log('🏠 Generating main portfolio page...');
      this.generateMainPortfolio(projects);
      
      // Generate summary
      const totalImages = Object.values(projects).reduce((sum, project) => sum + project.images.length, 0);
      console.log(`\n✅ Gallery generation complete!`);
      console.log(`📊 Summary:`);
      console.log(`   - Total images: ${totalImages}`);
      console.log(`   - Projects: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length}`);
      console.log(`   - Generated files: ${Object.keys(projects).filter(key => projects[key].images.length > 0).length + 1} HTML files`);
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error during gallery generation:', error);
    }
  }
}

// Run the generator if this script is executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new SimpleGalleryGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = SimpleGalleryGenerator;
```

```javascript
        <h1 class="display-4">${project.name}</h1>
        <p class="lead">UX Design Case Study</p>
        <hr class="lines">
        <div class="row text-center mt-4">
          <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="stats-card">
              <i class="fas fa-images fa-2x text-primary mb-2"></i>
              <p class="mb-0">${project.images.length} Deliverables</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="stats-card">
              <i class="fas fa-lightbulb fa-2x text-primary mb-2"></i>
              <p class="mb-0">User-Centered Design</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="stats-card">
              <i class="fas fa-chart-line fa-2x text-primary mb-2"></i>
              <p class="mb-0">Impactful Solutions</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="container py-5">
    ${this.generateGalleryHTML(projectKey, project)}

    <div class="text-center mt-5">
      <a href="../index.html" class="btn btn-primary">
        <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Portfolio
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lightbox2/2.11.4/js/lightbox.min.js"></script>
  <script>
    // Auto-playing testimonial gallery
    const testimonials = document.querySelectorAll('.testimonial-slide');
    let currentIndex = 0;

    function showTestimonial(index) {
      testimonials.forEach((slide, i) => {
        slide.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
      });
    }

    function nextTestimonial() {
      currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % testimonials.length;
      showTestimonial(currentIndex);
    }

    // Change testimonial every 4 seconds
    setInterval(nextTestimonial, 4000);
  </script>
</body>
</html>`;

    const outputPath = path.join(this.caseStudiesDir, `${projectKey}-gallery.html`);
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, template);
    console.log(`✅ Generated: ${projectKey}-gallery.html (${project.images.length} images)`);
  }
```

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class CarouselGenerator {
  constructor() {
    this.baseDir = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    this.logosDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'assets', 'images', 'logos');
    this.outputDir = path.join(this.baseDir, 'components');
    
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.outputDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.outputDir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  generateCarouselHTML() {
    const logos = [
      { name: 'Disney', file: 'DISNEYLOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'AT&T', file: 'ATTLOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'Microsoft', file: 'MSFTLOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'Microsoft Office', file: 'MSFTOFFICE.svg' },
      { name: 'Office 365', file: 'OFFICE365LOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'POP', file: 'POPLOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'T-Mobile', file: 'TMOBILELOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'Travelport', file: 'TRAVELPORTLOGO.svg' },
      { name: 'Volt', file: 'VOLTLOGO.svg' }
    ];

    let html = `<!-- Client Logo Carousel -->
<section class="client-carousel-section">
  <div class="carousel-container">
    <div class="carousel-track">
`;

    // Add logos twice for seamless loop
    [...logos, ...logos].forEach(logo => {
      html += `
      <div class="carousel-item">
        <img src="assets/images/logos/${logo.file}" alt="${logo.name} Logo" class="client-logo">
      </div>`;
    });

    html += `
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<style>
.client-carousel-section {
  background: #ffffff;
  padding: 3rem 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  width: 100vw;
  margin-left: calc(-50vw + 50%);
  margin-right: calc(-50vw + 50%);
}

.carousel-container {
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.carousel-track {
  display: flex;
  animation: scroll 30s linear infinite;
  width: calc(200px * ${logos.length * 2});
}

.carousel-item {
  flex: 0 0 200px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 0 2rem;
}

.client-logo {
  max-width: 120px;
  max-height: 60px;
  width: auto;
  height: auto;
  filter: grayscale(100%);
  transition: filter 0.3s ease;
}

.client-logo:hover {
  filter: grayscale(0%);
}

@keyframes scroll {
  0% {
    transform: translateX(0);
  }
  100% {
    transform: translateX(calc(-200px * ${logos.length}));
  }
}

/* Pause animation on hover */
.carousel-container:hover .carousel-track {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

/* Responsive adjustments */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .carousel-item {
    flex: 0 0 150px;
    padding: 0 1rem;
  }
  
  .client-logo {
    max-width: 80px;
    max-height: 40px;
  }
  
  .carousel-track {
    width: calc(150px * ${logos.length * 2});
  }
  
  @keyframes scroll {
    0% {
      transform: translateX(0);
    }
    100% {
      transform: translateX(calc(-150px * ${logos.length}));
    }
  }
}
</style>`;

    return html;
  }

  generateAll() {
    console.log('🎠 Generating client logo carousel...');
    
    try {
      const carouselHTML = this.generateCarouselHTML();
      const outputPath = path.join(this.outputDir, 'client-carousel.html');
      fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, carouselHTML);
      console.log(`✅ Generated: components/client-carousel.html`);
      
      // Also create a JavaScript version for easy integration
      const jsVersion = `// Client Logo Carousel Component
function createClientCarousel() {
  return \`${carouselHTML.replace(/`/g, '\\`')}\`;
}

module.exports = { createClientCarousel };`;
      
      const jsOutputPath = path.join(this.outputDir, 'client-carousel.js');
      fs.writeFileSync(jsOutputPath, jsVersion);
      console.log(`✅ Generated: components/client-carousel.js`);
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Error generating carousel:', error);
    }
  }
}

if (require.main === module) {
  const generator = new CarouselGenerator();
  generator.generateAll();
}

module.exports = CarouselGenerator;
```

Based on your Figma design vision and requirements, I've successfully implemented the following improvements:

### ✅ **Created Auto-Playing Testimonial Gallery**

  - OFFICE365LOGO.svg, POPLOGO.svg, TMOBILELOGO.svg, TRAVELPORTLOGO.svg, VOLTLOGO.svg

  - **T-Mobile**: 83 design deliverables  
  - **Disney**: 16 design deliverables
  - **BMGF**: 17 design deliverables

```javascript
  getProjectBackground(projectName) {
    const backgrounds = {
      'atmosfx': 'assets/images/backgrounds/atmosfx.webp',
      'tmobile': 'assets/images/backgrounds/tmobile-purchase.webp',
      'disney': 'assets/images/backgrounds/gates.webp', // Using gates as Disney background
      'bmgf': 'assets/images/backgrounds/gates.webp',
      'microsoft': 'assets/images/backgrounds/O365.webp',
      'att': 'assets/images/backgrounds/att.webp',
      'officelive': 'assets/images/backgrounds/officelive.webp',
      'tmobile-labs': 'assets/images/backgrounds/tmobile-labs.webp'
    };
    
    // Find matching background based on project name
    for (const [key, background] of Object.entries(backgrounds)) {
      if (projectName.toLowerCase().includes(key.toLowerCase())) {
        return background;
      }
    }
    
    // Default background for other projects
    return 'assets/images/backgrounds/atmosfx.webp';
  }
```

Now let me add the carousel CSS to the end of the file:


```css
/* Client Logo Carousel */
.client-carousel-section {
  background: #ffffff;
  padding: 3rem 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  width: 100vw;
  margin-left: calc(-50vw + 50%);
  margin-right: calc(-50vw + 50%);
}

.carousel-container {
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.carousel-track {
  display: flex;
  animation: scroll 30s linear infinite;
  width: calc(200px * 18);
}

.carousel-item {
  flex: 0 0 200px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 0 2rem;
}

.client-logo {
  max-width: 120px;
  max-height: 60px;
  width: auto;
  height: auto;
  filter: grayscale(100%);
  transition: filter 0.3s ease;
}

.client-logo:hover {
  filter: grayscale(0%);
}

@keyframes scroll {
  0% {
    transform: translateX(0);
  }
  100% {
    transform: translateX(calc(-200px * 9));
  }
}

/* Pause animation on hover */
.carousel-container:hover .carousel-track {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

/* Responsive adjustments */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .carousel-item {
    flex: 0 0 150px;
    padding: 0 1rem;
  }
  
  .client-logo {
    max-width: 80px;
    max-height: 40px;
  }
  
  .carousel-track {
    width: calc(150px * 18);
  }
  
  @keyframes scroll {
    0% {
      transform: translateX(0);
    }
    100% {
      transform: translateX(calc(-150px * 9));
    }
  }
}
```

## 1. Fix Netlify Configuration


The issue is that the script path is wrong. Since we're publishing from `src`, the script should be in the root. Let me fix this:


```plaintext
[build]
  publish = "src"
  command = "node scripts/simple-gallery.js"

->

[build]
  publish = "src"
  command = "node ../scripts/simple-gallery.js"
```
